Zetahyperbolic.ORIGINAL_EXACT_ZERO_FREE_DISK (p : Zetahyperbolic.HankelParams) (k : ℕ) (hk : 1 ≤ k) :
  ∃ radius,
    0 < radius ∧
      (∀ (z : ℂ),
          Polynomial.eval₂ (algebraMap ℚ ℂ) z (Zetahyperbolic.Delta_poly p k) = 0 →
            z.im = 0 ∧
              z ≠ ↑(ManuscriptChain.Real.zeta (2 * k + 1)) ∧ radius ≤ ‖z - ↑(ManuscriptChain.Real.zeta (2 * k + 1))‖) ∧
        ∃ z,
          Polynomial.eval₂ (algebraMap ℚ ℂ) z (Zetahyperbolic.Delta_poly p k) = 0 ∧
            ‖z - ↑(ManuscriptChain.Real.zeta (2 * k + 1))‖ = radius

    ∑ r ∈ rs, Polynomial.C (v r * -(↑r ^ (2 * k + 1))⁻¹)

LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.manuscript_reciprocal_moments (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters)
  (hp : ManuscriptChain.Real.Admissible p) (q : ℚ)
  (hq : ManuscriptChain.Real.evalQ (ManuscriptChain.Real.delta p) ↑q ≠ 0) (n : ℕ) :
  (∀ (i : Fin p.h),
      0 <
        LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.reciprocalAtom (LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.manuscriptRoots p hp) (↑q) i) ∧
    (LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.reciprocalHankel (LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.manuscriptRoots p hp) (↑q)
          n).PosSemidef ∧
      (Matrix.PosSemidef fun i j =>
          LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.reciprocalEvenMoment (LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.manuscriptRoots p hp)
            (↑q) (↑i + ↑j + 1)) ∧
        (0 <
              (LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.reciprocalHankel (LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.manuscriptRoots p hp)
                  (↑q) n).det ↔
            n ≤
              (LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.atomSupport
                  (LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.reciprocalAtom (LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.manuscriptRoots p hp)
                    ↑q)).card) ∧
          ((LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.atomSupport
                  (LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.reciprocalAtom (LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.manuscriptRoots p hp)
                    ↑q)).card <
              n →
            (LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.reciprocalHankel (LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.manuscriptRoots p hp)
                  (↑q) n).det =
              0)

  0 < (LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.reciprocalHankel r q n).det ↔
    n ≤ (LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.atomSupport (LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.reciprocalAtom r q)).card

  (ha : a ∈ Finset.Icc (p.N + 1) p.K) :
  ManuscriptChain.PartialFractions.residueCoeff p (i + j) a = ManuscriptChain.Real.residue p i j a
ManuscriptChain.PartialFractions.rational_kernel_decomposition (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters) (i j : ℕ) :
  ManuscriptChain.Real.numerator p i j =
    ManuscriptChain.Real.quotient p i j * ManuscriptChain.Real.tailD p +
      ∑ a ∈ Finset.Icc (p.N + 1) p.K,
        Polynomial.C (ManuscriptChain.Real.residue p i j a) *
          ∏ b ∈ (Finset.Icc (p.N + 1) p.K).erase a, (Polynomial.X + Polynomial.C (↑b ^ 2))
LeanPaper.RebuildKernelBridge.resolvedKernel_eq_fraction_all_real (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters) (i j : ℕ)
  (y : ℝ) :
  ManuscriptChain.Hyperbolic.resolvedKernel p i j y =
    Polynomial.eval₂ (algebraMap ℚ ℝ) (y ^ 2) (ManuscriptChain.Real.numerator p i j) /
      Polynomial.eval₂ (algebraMap ℚ ℝ) (y ^ 2) (ManuscriptChain.Real.tailD p)
LeanPaper.RebuildKernelBridge.gram_eq_fraction_integral (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters) (hk : 0 < p.k)
  (i j : Fin p.h) :
  ManuscriptChain.Real.gram p (ManuscriptChain.Real.zeta p.weight) i j =
    ∫ (y : ℝ) in Set.Ioi 0,
      Polynomial.eval₂ (algebraMap ℚ ℝ) (y ^ 2) (ManuscriptChain.Real.numerator p ↑i ↑j) /
          Polynomial.eval₂ (algebraMap ℚ ℝ) (y ^ 2) (ManuscriptChain.Real.tailD p) *
        ManuscriptChain.Hyperbolic.density p.k y

PaperOrder.PrincipalTheorems.independent_coefficients_full_hyperbolicity {h K : ℕ} (c : Fin h → Fin (K + 1) → ℝ)
  (constant : Matrix (Fin h) (Fin h) ℝ) (hconstant : constant.IsSymm)
  (hc : PaperOrder.PrincipalTheorems.coefficientRowsSeparate c) :
  ∃ beta,
    (Matrix.det fun i j =>
        Polynomial.C (constant i j) +
          Polynomial.X * Polynomial.C (PaperOrder.PrincipalTheorems.coefficientSlope c i j)) =
      Polynomial.C (PaperOrder.PrincipalTheorems.coefficientSlope c).det * ∏ i, (Polynomial.X + Polynomial.C (beta i))

  (c : Fin h → Fin (K + 1) → ℚ) :
  (ManuscriptChain.Real.dualDelta s (PaperOrder.CoreAlgebra.dualRowChange A c)).coeff h =
    A.det ^ 2 * (ManuscriptChain.Real.dualDelta s c).coeff h
PaperOrder.CoreAlgebra.dualDelta_degree_rowChange {h K : ℕ} (s : ℕ) (A : Matrix (Fin h) (Fin h) ℚ)
  (c : Fin h → Fin (K + 1) → ℚ) (hA : A.det ≠ 0) (hc : (ManuscriptChain.Real.dualDelta s c).coeff h ≠ 0) :
  (ManuscriptChain.Real.dualDelta s (PaperOrder.CoreAlgebra.dualRowChange A c)).natDegree = h

  (hslope : (PaperOrder.CoreAlgebra.dualSlopeMatrix c).det ≠ 0) : (ManuscriptChain.Real.dualDelta s c).natDegree = h
PaperOrder.CoreAlgebra.dualDelta_topCoeff_nonzero_rowChange_iff {h K : ℕ} (s : ℕ) (A : Matrix (Fin h) (Fin h) ℚ)
  (c : Fin h → Fin (K + 1) → ℚ) (hA : A.det ≠ 0) :
  (ManuscriptChain.Real.dualDelta s (PaperOrder.CoreAlgebra.dualRowChange A c)).coeff h ≠ 0 ↔

PaperOrder.CoreAlgebra.dualTwoByTwo_det_expansion (a b c u v w x : ℝ) :
  !![a + x * u, b + x * v; b + x * v, c + x * w].det =
    (u * w - v ^ 2) * x ^ 2 + (a * w + c * u - 2 * b * v) * x + (a * c - b ^ 2)
PaperOrder.CoreAlgebra.dualTwoByTwo_discriminant_sos (a b c u v w : ℝ) :
  u ^ 2 * PaperOrder.CoreAlgebra.dualTwoByTwoDiscriminant a b c u v w =
    (a * (u * w - v ^ 2) - c * u ^ 2 + 2 * b * u * v - a * v ^ 2) ^ 2 + 4 * (u * w - v ^ 2) * (b * u - a * v) ^ 2
PaperOrder.CoreAlgebra.dualTwoByTwo_discriminant_nonneg (a b c u v w : ℝ) (hu : 0 < u) (hM : 0 < u * w - v ^ 2) :
  0 ≤ PaperOrder.CoreAlgebra.dualTwoByTwoDiscriminant a b c u v w
PaperOrder.CoreAlgebra.dualTwoByTwo_discriminant_pos_of_nonproportional (a b c u v w : ℝ) (hu : 0 < u)
  (hM : 0 < u * w - v ^ 2) (hsep : b * u - a * v ≠ 0) : 0 < PaperOrder.CoreAlgebra.dualTwoByTwoDiscriminant a b c u v w
PaperOrder.CoreAlgebra.realQuadratic_has_root_of_discriminant_nonneg (A B C : ℝ) (hA : A ≠ 0)
  (hdisc : 0 ≤ B ^ 2 - 4 * A * C) : ∃ x, A * x ^ 2 + B * x + C = 0
PaperOrder.CoreAlgebra.dualTwoByTwo_det_has_real_zero (a b c u v w : ℝ) (hu : 0 < u) (hM : 0 < u * w - v ^ 2) :
  ∃ x, !![a + x * u, b + x * v; b + x * v, c + x * w].det = 0
PaperOrder.PrincipalTheorems.weightSeven_binomial (u v : ℝ) :
  (u + v) ^ 5 = ∑ ν ∈ Finset.range 6, ↑(Nat.choose 5 ν) * u ^ ν * v ^ (5 - ν)
PaperOrder.PrincipalTheorems.weightSeven_Krein_bilinear (a b : ℕ → ℝ) :
  PaperOrder.PrincipalTheorems.weightSevenPairing a b = PaperOrder.PrincipalTheorems.weightSevenKreinPairing a b
PaperOrder.PrincipalTheorems.weightSeven_Krein_quadratic (a : ℕ → ℝ) :
  PaperOrder.PrincipalTheorems.weightSevenPairing a a =
    1 / 1440 * ∑ ν ∈ Finset.range 3, ↑(Nat.choose 5 ν) * ((a ν + a (5 - ν)) ^ 2 - (a ν - a (5 - ν)) ^ 2)
PaperOrder.PrincipalTheorems.weightSeven_Krein_infinite_modes (a b : ℕ → ℕ → ℝ)
  (hsum : Summable fun m => PaperOrder.PrincipalTheorems.weightSevenPairing (a m) (b m)) :
  (Summable fun m => PaperOrder.PrincipalTheorems.weightSevenKreinPairing (a m) (b m)) ∧
    ∑' (m : ℕ), PaperOrder.PrincipalTheorems.weightSevenPairing (a m) (b m) =
      ∑' (m : ℕ), PaperOrder.PrincipalTheorems.weightSevenKreinPairing (a m) (b m)

  (hminus : ∀ (i : Fin h) (r : Fin (K + 1)), minus i r = 0)
  (hplus : PaperOrder.PrincipalTheorems.coefficientRowsSeparate plus) :
  (PaperOrder.PrincipalTheorems.finiteKreinGram plus minus).PosDef
PaperOrder.PrincipalTheorems.finiteKreinGram_neg_posDef {h K : ℕ} (plus minus : Fin h → Fin (K + 1) → ℝ)
  (hplus : ∀ (i : Fin h) (r : Fin (K + 1)), plus i r = 0)
  (hminus : PaperOrder.PrincipalTheorems.coefficientRowsSeparate minus) :
  (-PaperOrder.PrincipalTheorems.finiteKreinGram plus minus).PosDef

Zetahyperbolic.TriplePoleRepair.hermite_congruence {n : ℕ} (nodes : Fin n → ℚ) (c u : Fin n → Fin 3 → ℚ)
  (basis : Zetahyperbolic.TriplePoleRepair.JetIndex n → Polynomial ℚ) :
  Zetahyperbolic.TriplePoleRepair.momentMatrix nodes c u basis =
    (Zetahyperbolic.TriplePoleRepair.jetTable nodes basis *
        Matrix.blockDiagonal fun a =>
          Zetahyperbolic.TriplePoleRepair.localBlock (Zetahyperbolic.TriplePoleRepair.jetWeights (c a) (u a))) *
      (Zetahyperbolic.TriplePoleRepair.jetTable nodes basis).transpose

  (hpole : ∀ (a : Fin n), u a 0 ≠ 0) : (Zetahyperbolic.TriplePoleRepair.momentMatrix nodes c u basis).det ≠ 0

PaperChronology.Paper10.PAPER10_BETA_VANDERMONDE_RESIDUAL_SIGN (j N : ℕ) (h : N ≤ j) :
  (-1) ^ j * (-1) ^ (j - N) = (-1) ^ N

PaperChronology.Paper10.PAPER10_BETA_VANDERMONDE_ODD_BASE (j t : ℕ) (h : 2 * t + 1 ≤ j) :

  (h00 : a + u * b + s * c + u * s * d = 0) (h01 : a + u * b + t * c + u * t * d = 0)

      w11 * (a + v * b + t * c + v * t * d) ^ 2

PaperChronology.Paper10.PAPER10_DIFFERENTIAL_SHIFT_POLE_ALIGNMENT (j k m : ℕ) (hm : m ≤ k) :
  ↑j ^ (2 * (k - m)) * ↑j ^ (2 * m) = ↑j ^ (2 * k)

  3 * LeanPaper.Section3Newton.cubicSym1 a b c * LeanPaper.Section3Newton.cubicSym3 a b c ≤
    LeanPaper.Section3Newton.cubicSym2 a b c ^ 2
PaperAdvances.Theorem3_7.cubic_first_turan_nonnegative (x a b c : ℝ) :
  0 ≤
    ((x - b) * (x - c) + (x - a) * (x - c) + (x - a) * (x - b)) ^ 2 -
      (x - a) * (x - b) * (x - c) * (2 * (x - a + (x - b) + (x - c)))
PaperAdvances.Theorem3_7.cubic_second_turan_nonnegative (x a b c : ℝ) :
  0 ≤ (2 * (x - a + (x - b) + (x - c))) ^ 2 - 6 * ((x - a) * (x - b) + (x - a) * (x - c) + (x - b) * (x - c))
PaperAdvances.Theorem3_7.three_mode_taylor_bound (a b c : ℝ) : 3 * (2 * (a * b + a * c + b * c)) ≤ 2 * (a + b + c) ^ 2

  (PaperAdvances.Theorem3_8_Jacobi.unitDetPoly A).coeff 1 = A.trace
PaperAdvances.Theorem3_8_ExactTaylor.affine_det_exact_second_order {n : ℕ} (G H : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) ℝ)
  (hG : G.det ≠ 0) (t : ℝ) :
  (G + t • H).det =
    G.det *
      (1 + (G⁻¹ * H).trace * t +
        Polynomial.eval t (PaperAdvances.Theorem3_8_Jacobi.unitDetPoly (G⁻¹ * H)).divX.divX * t ^ 2)

LeanPaper.Section3ContinuedFractions.corollary_3_5_normalized_two_det (a b c t : ℝ) :
  Matrix.det ![![1 + t * a, t * b], ![t * b, 1 + t * c]] = LeanPaper.Section3ContinuedFractions.normalizedTwoDet a b c t
LeanPaper.Section3ContinuedFractions.corollary_3_5_two_level_schur_identity (a b c t : ℝ) (h : 1 + t * a ≠ 0) :
  (1 + t * a) * (1 + t * c - (t * b) ^ 2 / (1 + t * a)) = LeanPaper.Section3ContinuedFractions.normalizedTwoDet a b c t
LeanPaper.Section3ContinuedFractions.normalized_three_continuant_recurrence (a b c d e t : ℝ) :
  LeanPaper.Section3ContinuedFractions.normalizedThreeContinuant a b c d e t =
    (1 + t * e) * ((1 + t * a) * (1 + t * c) - (t * b) ^ 2) - (t * d) ^ 2 * (1 + t * a)
LeanPaper.Section3ContinuedFractions.normalized_three_schur_identity (a b c d e t : ℝ) (h1 : 1 + t * a ≠ 0)
  (h2 : 1 + t * c - (t * b) ^ 2 / (1 + t * a) ≠ 0) :
  (1 + t * a) * (1 + t * c - (t * b) ^ 2 / (1 + t * a)) *
      (1 + t * e - (t * d) ^ 2 / (1 + t * c - (t * b) ^ 2 / (1 + t * a))) =
    LeanPaper.Section3ContinuedFractions.normalizedThreeContinuant a b c d e t
LeanPaper.Section3ContinuedFractions.normalized_three_continuant_eq_det (a b c d e t : ℝ) :
  LeanPaper.Section3ContinuedFractions.normalizedThreeContinuant a b c d e t =
    !![1 + t * a, t * b, 0; t * b, 1 + t * c, t * d; 0, t * d, 1 + t * e].det

PaperAdvances.Corollary3_6_PowerSumExtrema.positive_atomic_moment_bounds {h : ℕ} (u : Fin h → ℝ)
  (hu : ∀ (i : Fin h), 0 ≤ u i) (j : Fin h) (hj : ∀ (i : Fin h), u i ≤ u j) (r : ℕ) :

LeanPaper.Section3RootMomentBounds.actual_reciprocal_moment_bounds (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters)
  (hp : ManuscriptChain.Real.Admissible p) (q : ℚ)
  (hq : ManuscriptChain.Real.evalQ (ManuscriptChain.Real.delta p) ↑q ≠ 0) :
  ∃ j,
    0 < LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.reciprocalAtom (LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.manuscriptRoots p hp) (↑q) j ∧
      (∀ (i : Fin p.h),
          LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.reciprocalAtom (LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.manuscriptRoots p hp) (↑q) i ≤
            LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.reciprocalAtom (LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.manuscriptRoots p hp) (↑q)
              j) ∧
        ∀ (m : ℕ),
          LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.reciprocalAtom (LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.manuscriptRoots p hp) (↑q) j ^
                m ≤
              LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.reciprocalEvenMoment
                (LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.manuscriptRoots p hp) (↑q) m ∧
            LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.reciprocalEvenMoment (LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.manuscriptRoots p hp)
                (↑q) m ≤
              ↑p.h *
                LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.reciprocalAtom (LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.manuscriptRoots p hp)
                    (↑q) j ^
                  m

PaperAdvances.Proposition4_3_ComplexPadicDisk.PROPOSITION_4_3_INTEGRAL_COMPLEX_PADIC {p : ℕ} [Fact (Nat.Prime p)]
  (Q : Polynomial ℤ_[p]) (ξ : ℤ_[p]) (α : ℂ_[p]) (hQ : Polynomial.eval ξ Q ≠ 0)
  (hα : ‖α - ↑((algebraMap ℚ_[p] (PadicAlgCl p)) ↑ξ)‖ < ‖↑(Polynomial.eval ξ Q)‖) :
  ‖Polynomial.eval α (Polynomial.map (algebraMap ℚ_[p] ℂ_[p]) (Polynomial.map PadicInt.Coe.ringHom Q))‖ =
      ‖↑(Polynomial.eval ξ Q)‖ ∧
    Polynomial.eval α (Polynomial.map (algebraMap ℚ_[p] ℂ_[p]) (Polynomial.map PadicInt.Coe.ringHom Q)) ≠ 0
PaperAdvances.Proposition4_3_ComplexPadicDisk.PROPOSITION_4_3_INTEGER_COMPLEX_PADIC {p : ℕ} [Fact (Nat.Prime p)]
  (Q : Polynomial ℤ) (ξ : ℤ_[p]) (α : ℂ_[p]) (hQ : Polynomial.eval ξ (Polynomial.map (Int.castRingHom ℤ_[p]) Q) ≠ 0)
  (hα :
    ‖α - ↑((algebraMap ℚ_[p] (PadicAlgCl p)) ↑ξ)‖ < ‖↑(Polynomial.eval ξ (Polynomial.map (Int.castRingHom ℤ_[p]) Q))‖) :
  ‖Polynomial.eval α
          (Polynomial.map (algebraMap ℚ_[p] ℂ_[p])
            (Polynomial.map PadicInt.Coe.ringHom (Polynomial.map (Int.castRingHom ℤ_[p]) Q)))‖ =
      ‖↑(Polynomial.eval ξ (Polynomial.map (Int.castRingHom ℤ_[p]) Q))‖ ∧
    Polynomial.eval α
        (Polynomial.map (algebraMap ℚ_[p] ℂ_[p])
          (Polynomial.map PadicInt.Coe.ringHom (Polynomial.map (Int.castRingHom ℤ_[p]) Q))) ≠
      0
PaperAdvances.Proposition4_3_IsometricExtension.ISOMETRIC_EXTENSION_UNIT_DISK.{u_1, u_2} {K : Type u_1} {L : Type u_2}
  [NontriviallyNormedField K] [IsUltrametricDist K] [NontriviallyNormedField L] [IsUltrametricDist L] (φ : K →+* L)
  (hφ : ∀ (y : K), ‖φ y‖ = ‖y‖) (P : Polynomial K) (z : L) (h0 : P.coeff 0 ≠ 0)
  (hcoeff : ∀ (k : ℕ), 0 < k → ‖P.coeff k‖ ≤ 1) (hcenter : ‖P.coeff 0‖ ≤ 1) (hz : ‖z‖ < ‖P.coeff 0‖) :
  ‖Polynomial.eval z (Polynomial.map φ P)‖ = ‖P.coeff 0‖ ∧ Polynomial.eval z (Polynomial.map φ P) ≠ 0
PaperAdvances.Proposition4_3_IsometricExtension.PROPOSITION_4_3_INTEGRAL_ISOMETRIC_EXTENSION.{u_1} {p : ℕ}
  [Fact (Nat.Prime p)] {L : Type u_1} [NontriviallyNormedField L] [IsUltrametricDist L] (φ : ℚ_[p] →+* L)
  (hφ : ∀ (y : ℚ_[p]), ‖φ y‖ = ‖y‖) (Q : Polynomial ℤ_[p]) (ξ : ℤ_[p]) (α : L) (hQ : Polynomial.eval ξ Q ≠ 0)
  (hα : ‖α - φ ↑ξ‖ < ‖↑(Polynomial.eval ξ Q)‖) :
  ‖Polynomial.eval α (Polynomial.map φ (Polynomial.map PadicInt.Coe.ringHom Q))‖ = ‖↑(Polynomial.eval ξ Q)‖ ∧
    Polynomial.eval α (Polynomial.map φ (Polynomial.map PadicInt.Coe.ringHom Q)) ≠ 0
PaperAdvances.Proposition4_3_PadicDisk.finite_ultrametric_sum_strict.{u_1, u_2} {K : Type u_1}
  [NontriviallyNormedField K] [IsUltrametricDist K] {ι : Type u_2} [DecidableEq ι] (s : Finset ι) (f : ι → K) (B : ℝ)
  (hB : 0 < B) (hf : ∀ i ∈ s, ‖f i‖ < B) : ‖∑ i ∈ s, f i‖ < B
PaperAdvances.Proposition4_3_PadicDisk.ultrametric_eval_norm_eq_constant.{u_1} {K : Type u_1}
  [NontriviallyNormedField K] [IsUltrametricDist K] (P : Polynomial K) (x : K) (h0 : P.coeff 0 ≠ 0)
  (hterms : ∀ (k : ℕ), 0 < k → ‖P.coeff k * x ^ k‖ < ‖P.coeff 0‖) : ‖Polynomial.eval x P‖ = ‖P.coeff 0‖
PaperAdvances.Proposition4_3_PadicDisk.ultrametric_unit_coeff_disk.{u_1} {K : Type u_1} [NontriviallyNormedField K]
  [IsUltrametricDist K] (P : Polynomial K) (x : K) (h0 : P.coeff 0 ≠ 0) (hcoeff : ∀ (k : ℕ), 0 < k → ‖P.coeff k‖ ≤ 1)
  (hcenter : ‖P.coeff 0‖ ≤ 1) (hx : ‖x‖ < ‖P.coeff 0‖) : ‖Polynomial.eval x P‖ = ‖P.coeff 0‖ ∧ Polynomial.eval x P ≠ 0
PaperAdvances.Proposition4_3_PadicDisk.PROPOSITION_4_3_INTEGRAL_QPADIC {p : ℕ} [Fact (Nat.Prime p)]
  (Q : Polynomial ℤ_[p]) (ξ : ℤ_[p]) (α : ℚ_[p]) (hQ : Polynomial.eval ξ Q ≠ 0)
  (hα : ‖α - ↑ξ‖ < ‖↑(Polynomial.eval ξ Q)‖) :
  ‖Polynomial.eval α (Polynomial.map PadicInt.Coe.ringHom Q)‖ = ‖↑(Polynomial.eval ξ Q)‖ ∧
    Polynomial.eval α (Polynomial.map PadicInt.Coe.ringHom Q) ≠ 0
PaperAdvances.Proposition4_3_PadicDisk.PROPOSITION_4_3_INTEGER_QPADIC {p : ℕ} [Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (Q : Polynomial ℤ)
  (ξ : ℤ_[p]) (α : ℚ_[p]) (hQ : Polynomial.eval ξ (Polynomial.map (Int.castRingHom ℤ_[p]) Q) ≠ 0)
  (hα : ‖α - ↑ξ‖ < ‖↑(Polynomial.eval ξ (Polynomial.map (Int.castRingHom ℤ_[p]) Q))‖) :
  ‖Polynomial.eval α (Polynomial.map PadicInt.Coe.ringHom (Polynomial.map (Int.castRingHom ℤ_[p]) Q))‖ =
      ‖↑(Polynomial.eval ξ (Polynomial.map (Int.castRingHom ℤ_[p]) Q))‖ ∧
    Polynomial.eval α (Polynomial.map PadicInt.Coe.ringHom (Polynomial.map (Int.castRingHom ℤ_[p]) Q)) ≠ 0
PaperOrder.Section4.proposition_4_8_distance_product_nonnegative (ξ : ℝ) (xs : List ℝ) :
  0 ≤ (List.map (fun r => |ξ - r|) xs).prod
PaperOrder.Section4.proposition_4_8_distance_product_lower (ξ δ : ℝ) (xs : List ℝ) (hδ : 0 ≤ δ) :
  (∀ r ∈ xs, δ ≤ |ξ - r|) → δ ^ xs.length ≤ (List.map (fun r => |ξ - r|) xs).prod
PaperOrder.Section4.proposition_4_8_distance_product_upper (ξ η : ℝ) (xs : List ℝ) (hη : 0 ≤ η) :
  (∀ r ∈ xs, |ξ - r| ≤ η) → (List.map (fun r => |ξ - r|) xs).prod ≤ η ^ xs.length
PaperOrder.Section4.proposition_4_8_factor_evaluation_bounds (ξ c δ η : ℝ) (xs : List ℝ) (hδ : 0 ≤ δ) (hη : 0 ≤ η)
  (hlower : ∀ r ∈ xs, δ ≤ |ξ - r|) (hupper : ∀ r ∈ xs, |ξ - r| ≤ η) :
  |c| * δ ^ xs.length ≤ |c| * (List.map (fun r => |ξ - r|) xs).prod ∧
    |c| * (List.map (fun r => |ξ - r|) xs).prod ≤ |c| * η ^ xs.length
PaperOrder.Section4.theorem_4_6_integer_leading_coefficient_barrier (c : ℤ) (ξvalue detValue : ℝ) (hc : c ≠ 0)
  (hidentity : ↑|c| = |ξvalue| * |detValue|) : 1 ≤ |ξvalue| * |detValue|
PaperOrder.Section4.theorem_4_6_integer_evaluation_lower_bound (c : ℤ) (ξvalue detValue T : ℝ) (hc : c ≠ 0)
  (hidentity : ↑|c| = |ξvalue| * |detValue|) (hdet : |detValue| ≤ T) : 1 ≤ |ξvalue| * T
PaperOrder.Section4.theorem_4_6_reciprocal_evaluation_lower_bound (c : ℤ) (ξvalue detValue T : ℝ) (hc : c ≠ 0)
  (hidentity : ↑|c| = |ξvalue| * |detValue|) (hT : 0 < T) (hdet : |detValue| ≤ T) : 1 / T ≤ |ξvalue|
PaperOrder.Section4.theorem_4_7_finite_cluster_sum_bound (xs : List ℝ) (U τ : ℝ) :
  (∀ x ∈ xs, x ≤ U) → xs.sum ≤ τ * ↑xs.length + (U - τ) * ↑(PaperOrder.Section4.countAtLeastThreshold τ xs)
PaperOrder.Section4.theorem_4_7_cluster_fraction_lower_bound (xs : List ℝ) (U τ μ : ℝ) (hU : τ < U)
  (hcap : ∀ x ∈ xs, x ≤ U) (haverage : μ * ↑xs.length ≤ xs.sum) :
  (μ - τ) / (U - τ) * ↑xs.length ≤ ↑(PaperOrder.Section4.countAtLeastThreshold τ xs)
PaperOrder.Section4.theorem_4_7_cluster_percentage_arithmetic :
  14 / 1000 < (2589 / 10000 - 1 / 10) / (11411 / 1000 - 1 / 10)
LeanPaper.Section2FiniteDifference.tau_monomial_difference (k d : ℕ) (hk : 1 ≤ k) :
  ∑ i ∈ Finset.range (d + 1), ↑(d.choose i) * Zetahyperbolic.tauMonomial k i - Zetahyperbolic.tauMonomial k d =
    if d = 2 * k then 1 else 0
LeanPaper.Section2FiniteDifference.finite_span_difference (k : ℕ) (hk : 1 ≤ k) (es : Finset ℕ) (u : ℕ → ℚ)
  (rs : Finset ℤ) (v : ℤ → ℚ) (hr : ∀ r ∈ rs, r ≠ 0 ∧ r ≠ 1) :
  LeanPaper.Section2FiniteDifference.translatedMoment k es u rs v -
      LeanPaper.PullbackReflection.tauFiniteSpan k es u rs v =
    ∑ d ∈ es, Polynomial.C (u d * if d = 2 * k then 1 else 0) + ∑ r ∈ rs, Polynomial.C (v r * -(↑r ^ (2 * k + 1))⁻¹)
LeanPaper.Section2FiniteDifference.finite_span_difference_coefficient (k : ℕ) (hk : 1 ≤ k) (es : Finset ℕ) (u : ℕ → ℚ)
  (rs : Finset ℤ) (v : ℤ → ℚ) (hr : ∀ r ∈ rs, r ≠ 0 ∧ r ≠ 1) :
  LeanPaper.Section2FiniteDifference.translatedMoment k es u rs v -
      LeanPaper.PullbackReflection.tauFiniteSpan k es u rs v =
    Polynomial.C (if 2 * k ∈ es then u (2 * k) else 0) + ∑ r ∈ rs, Polynomial.C (v r * -(↑r ^ (2 * k + 1))⁻¹)
LeanPaper.Section2FiniteDifference.finite_span_translation (es : Finset ℕ) (u : ℕ → ℚ) (rs : Finset ℤ) (v : ℤ → ℚ)
  (x : ℚ) :
  LeanPaper.Section2FiniteDifference.finiteSpanValue es u rs v (x + 1) =
    LeanPaper.Section2FiniteDifference.translatedSpanValue es u rs v x
LeanPaper.Section2FiniteDifference.function_and_moment_difference (k : ℕ) (hk : 1 ≤ k) (es : Finset ℕ) (u : ℕ → ℚ)
  (rs : Finset ℤ) (v : ℤ → ℚ) (hr : ∀ r ∈ rs, r ≠ 0 ∧ r ≠ 1) (x : ℚ) :
  LeanPaper.Section2FiniteDifference.finiteSpanValue es u rs v (x + 1) =
      LeanPaper.Section2FiniteDifference.translatedSpanValue es u rs v x ∧
    LeanPaper.Section2FiniteDifference.translatedMoment k es u rs v -
        LeanPaper.PullbackReflection.tauFiniteSpan k es u rs v =
      Polynomial.C (if 2 * k ∈ es then u (2 * k) else 0) + ∑ r ∈ rs, Polynomial.C (v r * -(↑r ^ (2 * k + 1))⁻¹)
Zetahyperbolic.lagrangePolePolynomial_eval_self (N K a : ℕ) (ha : a ∈ Finset.Icc (N + 1) K) :
  Polynomial.eval (-↑a ^ 2) (Zetahyperbolic.lagrangePolePolynomial N K a) = 1
Zetahyperbolic.lagrangePolePolynomial_eval_ne (N K a b : ℕ) (ha : a ∈ Finset.Icc (N + 1) K)
  (hb : b ∈ Finset.Icc (N + 1) K) (hab : a ≠ b) :
  Polynomial.eval (-↑b ^ 2) (Zetahyperbolic.lagrangePolePolynomial N K a) = 0
Zetahyperbolic.lagrangePolePolynomial_eval (N K a b : ℕ) (ha : a ∈ Finset.Icc (N + 1) K)
  (hb : b ∈ Finset.Icc (N + 1) K) :
  Polynomial.eval (-↑b ^ 2) (Zetahyperbolic.lagrangePolePolynomial N K a) = if a = b then 1 else 0
Zetahyperbolic.lagrangePolePolynomial_eval_eq_one_iff (N K a b : ℕ) (ha : a ∈ Finset.Icc (N + 1) K)
  (hb : b ∈ Finset.Icc (N + 1) K) : Polynomial.eval (-↑b ^ 2) (Zetahyperbolic.lagrangePolePolynomial N K a) = 1 ↔ a = b
Zetahyperbolic.lagrangePoleEvaluationMatrix_eq_one (N K : ℕ) : Zetahyperbolic.lagrangePoleEvaluationMatrix N K = 1
Zetahyperbolic.det_lagrangePoleEvaluationMatrix (N K : ℕ) : (Zetahyperbolic.lagrangePoleEvaluationMatrix N K).det = 1
Zetahyperbolic.lagrangePoleEvaluationMatrix_det_ne_zero (N K : ℕ) :
  (Zetahyperbolic.lagrangePoleEvaluationMatrix N K).det ≠ 0
Zetahyperbolic.lagrangePoleEvaluationMatrix_mulVec (N K : ℕ) (v : ↥(Finset.Icc (N + 1) K) → ℚ) :
  (Zetahyperbolic.lagrangePoleEvaluationMatrix N K).mulVec v = v
LeanPaper.Section2LagrangeBridge.polePolynomial_eval (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters) (a b : Fin p.h) :
  Polynomial.eval (ManuscriptChain.PoleDiagonalization.poleNode p b)
      (LeanPaper.Section2LagrangeBridge.polePolynomial p a) =
    if a = b then 1 else 0
LeanPaper.Section2LagrangeBridge.polePolynomial_natDegree (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters) (a : Fin p.h) :
  (LeanPaper.Section2LagrangeBridge.polePolynomial p a).natDegree = p.h - 1
LeanPaper.Section2LagrangeBridge.polePolynomial_ne_zero (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters) (a : Fin p.h) :
  LeanPaper.Section2LagrangeBridge.polePolynomial p a ≠ 0
LeanPaper.Section2LagrangeBridge.polePolynomial_eval_sum (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters) (a : Fin p.h) (x : ℚ) :
  Polynomial.eval x (LeanPaper.Section2LagrangeBridge.polePolynomial p a) =
    ∑ i, LeanPaper.Section2LagrangeBridge.coefficientMatrix p a i * x ^ ↑i
LeanPaper.Section2LagrangeBridge.coefficientMatrix_mul_vandermonde (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters) :
  LeanPaper.Section2LagrangeBridge.coefficientMatrix p * ManuscriptChain.PoleDiagonalization.poleVandermonde p = 1
LeanPaper.Section2LagrangeBridge.coefficientMatrix_eq_inverse (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters) :
  LeanPaper.Section2LagrangeBridge.coefficientMatrix p = (ManuscriptChain.PoleDiagonalization.poleVandermonde p)⁻¹
LeanPaper.Section2LagrangeBridge.coefficientMatrix_det_ne_zero (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters) :
  (LeanPaper.Section2LagrangeBridge.coefficientMatrix p).det ≠ 0
LeanPaper.Section2LagrangeBridge.lagrange_pencil_eq_actual (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters) :
  ManuscriptChain.BasisInvariance.changeBasisPencil (ManuscriptChain.Real.pencil p)
      (LeanPaper.Section2LagrangeBridge.coefficientMatrix p) (LeanPaper.Section2LagrangeBridge.coefficientMatrix p) =
    ManuscriptChain.PoleDiagonalization.poleDiagonalPencil p
LeanPaper.Section2LagrangeBridge.actual_lagrange_entries (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters) (a b : Fin p.h) :
  ManuscriptChain.PoleDiagonalization.poleDiagonalPencil p a b =
    ∑ i,
      ∑ j,
        Polynomial.C
            ((LeanPaper.Section2LagrangeBridge.polePolynomial p a).coeff ↑i *
              (LeanPaper.Section2LagrangeBridge.polePolynomial p b).coeff ↑j) *
          ManuscriptChain.Real.pencil p i j
LeanPaper.Section2LagrangeBridge.actual_lagrange_slope (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters) :
  LeanPaper.Section2LagrangeBridge.coefficientMatrix p *
        ManuscriptChain.PoleDiagonalization.rationalDerivativeMatrix p *
      (LeanPaper.Section2LagrangeBridge.coefficientMatrix p).transpose =
    Matrix.diagonal (ManuscriptChain.PoleDiagonalization.poleWeight p)
LeanPaper.Section2LagrangeIdentification.negative_square_injective : Function.Injective fun a => -↑a ^ 2
LeanPaper.Section2LagrangeIdentification.archive_polynomial_eq_mathlib (N K a : ℕ) :
  Zetahyperbolic.lagrangePolePolynomial N K a = Lagrange.basis (Finset.Icc (N + 1) K) (fun b => -↑b ^ 2) a
LeanPaper.Section2LagrangeIdentification.archive_polynomial_natDegree (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters)
  (a : Fin p.h) :
  (Zetahyperbolic.lagrangePolePolynomial p.N p.K (ManuscriptChain.PoleDiagonalization.poleIndex p a)).natDegree =
    p.h - 1
LeanPaper.Section2LagrangeIdentification.archive_polynomial_eval (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters) (a b : Fin p.h) :
  Polynomial.eval (ManuscriptChain.PoleDiagonalization.poleNode p b)
      (Zetahyperbolic.lagrangePolePolynomial p.N p.K (ManuscriptChain.PoleDiagonalization.poleIndex p a)) =
    if a = b then 1 else 0
LeanPaper.Section2LagrangeIdentification.archive_polynomial_eq_constructed (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters)
  (a : Fin p.h) :
  Zetahyperbolic.lagrangePolePolynomial p.N p.K (ManuscriptChain.PoleDiagonalization.poleIndex p a) =
    LeanPaper.Section2LagrangeBridge.polePolynomial p a
LeanPaper.Section2LagrangeIdentification.archive_coefficients_eq_inverse (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters)
  (a i : Fin p.h) :
  (Zetahyperbolic.lagrangePolePolynomial p.N p.K (ManuscriptChain.PoleDiagonalization.poleIndex p a)).coeff ↑i =
    (ManuscriptChain.PoleDiagonalization.poleVandermonde p)⁻¹ a i
LeanPaper.Section4ManuscriptBridge.primitive_leading_barrier_identity (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters)
  (hp : ManuscriptChain.Real.Admissible p) :
  ↑|(ManuscriptChain.GeneralIrrationality.primitivePolynomial p).leadingCoeff| =
    |ManuscriptChain.RealTransfer.evalReal (ManuscriptChain.GeneralIrrationality.primitivePolynomial p)
          (ManuscriptChain.Real.zeta p.weight)| *
      |1 / ManuscriptChain.LeadingCoefficientGap.determinantRatio p|
LeanPaper.Section4ManuscriptBridge.primitive_evaluation_lower_of_inverse_ratio_bound
  (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters) (hp : ManuscriptChain.Real.Admissible p) (T : ℝ) (hT : 0 < T)
  (hbound : |1 / ManuscriptChain.LeadingCoefficientGap.determinantRatio p| ≤ T) :
  1 / T ≤
    |ManuscriptChain.RealTransfer.evalReal (ManuscriptChain.GeneralIrrationality.primitivePolynomial p)
        (ManuscriptChain.Real.zeta p.weight)|
LeanPaper.Section4ManuscriptBridge.manuscript_spectral_cluster_fraction (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters)
  (hp : ManuscriptChain.Real.Admissible p) (U τ μ : ℝ) (hU : τ < U)
  (hcap : ∀ (i : Fin p.h), LeanPaper.Section4ManuscriptBridge.spectralExponent p hp i ≤ U)
  (haverage : μ * ↑p.h ≤ ∑ i, LeanPaper.Section4ManuscriptBridge.spectralExponent p hp i) :
  (μ - τ) / (U - τ) * ↑p.h ≤
    ↑(PaperOrder.Section4.countAtLeastThreshold τ (LeanPaper.Section4ManuscriptBridge.spectralExponentList p hp))
LeanPaper.Section4ManuscriptBridge.manuscript_large_exponent_gives_close_root (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters)
  (hp : ManuscriptChain.Real.Admissible p) (τ : ℝ) (i : Fin p.h)
  (hlarge : τ ≤ LeanPaper.Section4ManuscriptBridge.spectralExponent p hp i) :
  have r := (ManuscriptChain.General.factorization p ⋯).root i;
  ManuscriptChain.Real.evalQ (ManuscriptChain.Real.delta p) r = 0 ∧
    |r - ManuscriptChain.Real.zeta p.weight| ≤ Real.exp (-τ)
LeanPaper.Section4ManuscriptBridge.manuscript_primitive_complex_padic_disk (params : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters)
  {q : ℕ} [Fact (Nat.Prime q)] (ξ : ℤ_[q]) (α : ℂ_[q])
  (hQ :
    Polynomial.eval ξ
        (Polynomial.map (Int.castRingHom ℤ_[q]) (ManuscriptChain.GeneralIrrationality.primitivePolynomial params)) ≠
      0)
  (hα :
    ‖α - ↑((algebraMap ℚ_[q] (PadicAlgCl q)) ↑ξ)‖ <
      ‖↑(Polynomial.eval ξ
            (Polynomial.map (Int.castRingHom ℤ_[q])
              (ManuscriptChain.GeneralIrrationality.primitivePolynomial params)))‖) :
  ‖Polynomial.eval α
          (Polynomial.map (algebraMap ℚ_[q] ℂ_[q])
            (Polynomial.map PadicInt.Coe.ringHom
              (Polynomial.map (Int.castRingHom ℤ_[q])
                (ManuscriptChain.GeneralIrrationality.primitivePolynomial params))))‖ =
      ‖↑(Polynomial.eval ξ
            (Polynomial.map (Int.castRingHom ℤ_[q])
              (ManuscriptChain.GeneralIrrationality.primitivePolynomial params)))‖ ∧
    Polynomial.eval α
        (Polynomial.map (algebraMap ℚ_[q] ℂ_[q])
          (Polynomial.map PadicInt.Coe.ringHom
            (Polynomial.map (Int.castRingHom ℤ_[q])
              (ManuscriptChain.GeneralIrrationality.primitivePolynomial params)))) ≠
      0
LeanPaper.Section5DualSpectrum.actualDualPencil_polynomialMatrix {h K : ℕ} (s : ℕ) (c : Fin h → Fin (K + 1) → ℚ)

  (LeanPaper.Section5DualSpectrum.actualDualPencil s c hc).polynomialMatrix = fun i j =>

LeanPaper.HigherDerivativeTuran.iterated_turan_strict {P : Polynomial ℝ} (hP : P.Splits) (k : ℕ) (hk : k < P.natDegree)
  (x : ℝ) (hx : Polynomial.eval x ((⇑Polynomial.derivative)^[k] P) ≠ 0) :
  0 < PaperAdvances.Theorem3_7_AllDegrees.turan ((⇑Polynomial.derivative)^[k] P) x
LeanPaper.HigherDerivativeTuran.iterated_turan_eq_zero_iff {P : Polynomial ℝ} (hP : P.Splits) (k : ℕ)
  (hk : k < P.natDegree) (x : ℝ) :
  PaperAdvances.Theorem3_7_AllDegrees.turan ((⇑Polynomial.derivative)^[k] P) x = 0 ↔
    Polynomial.eval x ((⇑Polynomial.derivative)^[k] P) = 0 ∧
      Polynomial.eval x (Polynomial.derivative ((⇑Polynomial.derivative)^[k] P)) = 0

LeanPaper.HigherDerivativeTuran.primitiveRealPolynomial_identity (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters) :
  LeanPaper.HigherDerivativeTuran.primitiveRealPolynomial p =
    Polynomial.C ↑(ManuscriptChain.GeneralIrrationality.normalization p).scale *
      ManuscriptChain.Real.realPolynomial (ManuscriptChain.Real.delta p)

  (hp : ManuscriptChain.Real.Admissible p) : (LeanPaper.HigherDerivativeTuran.primitiveRealPolynomial p).Splits

  (LeanPaper.HigherDerivativeTuran.primitiveRealPolynomial p).natDegree = p.h

      ((⇑Polynomial.derivative)^[k] (LeanPaper.HigherDerivativeTuran.primitiveRealPolynomial p)) x
LeanPaper.HigherDerivativeTuran.actual_primitive_all_derivative_turan_equality (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters)
  (hp : ManuscriptChain.Real.Admissible p) (k : ℕ) (hk : k < p.h) (x : ℝ) :
  PaperAdvances.Theorem3_7_AllDegrees.turan
        ((⇑Polynomial.derivative)^[k] (LeanPaper.HigherDerivativeTuran.primitiveRealPolynomial p)) x =
      0 ↔
    Polynomial.eval x ((⇑Polynomial.derivative)^[k] (LeanPaper.HigherDerivativeTuran.primitiveRealPolynomial p)) = 0 ∧
      Polynomial.eval x
          (Polynomial.derivative
            ((⇑Polynomial.derivative)^[k] (LeanPaper.HigherDerivativeTuran.primitiveRealPolynomial p))) =
        0

        (LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.reciprocalHankel (LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.manuscriptRoots p hp) q

LeanPaper.ManuscriptObstructions.normalized_fraction_printed_formula_fails :
  (1 + 0 * 3) / LeanPaper.Section3ContinuedFractions.normalizedTwoDet 2 1 3 0 ≠ 1 / (2 + 0 - 1 ^ 2 / (3 + 0))
LeanPaper.ManuscriptObstructions.first_logarithmic_ratio_sign_fails :
  Polynomial.eval 0 (Polynomial.derivative (Polynomial.X - 1)) / Polynomial.eval 0 (Polynomial.X - 1) ≠ (1 - 0)⁻¹
LeanPaper.NormalizedNewton.laguerre (n : ℕ) (P : Polynomial ℝ) (x : ℝ) : ℝ
LeanPaper.NormalizedNewton.laguerre_mul_root_identity (m : ℕ) (P : Polynomial ℝ) (a x : ℝ) :
  ↑m * LeanPaper.NormalizedNewton.laguerre (m + 1) ((Polynomial.X - Polynomial.C a) * P) x =
    (↑m + 1) * (x - a) ^ 2 * LeanPaper.NormalizedNewton.laguerre m P x +
      ((x - a) * Polynomial.eval x (Polynomial.derivative P) - ↑m * Polynomial.eval x P) ^ 2
LeanPaper.NormalizedNewton.laguerre_nonnegative_of_real_splits {P : Polynomial ℝ} (hP : P.Splits) (x : ℝ) :
  0 ≤ LeanPaper.NormalizedNewton.laguerre P.natDegree P x
LeanPaper.NormalizedNewton.normalizedJet (P : Polynomial ℝ) (n k : ℕ) (x : ℝ) : ℝ
LeanPaper.NormalizedNewton.normalizedJet_newton {P : Polynomial ℝ} (hP : P.Splits) (k : ℕ) (hk : k + 2 ≤ P.natDegree)
  (x : ℝ) :
  LeanPaper.NormalizedNewton.normalizedJet P P.natDegree k x *
      LeanPaper.NormalizedNewton.normalizedJet P P.natDegree (k + 2) x ≤
    LeanPaper.NormalizedNewton.normalizedJet P P.natDegree (k + 1) x ^ 2
LeanPaper.NormalizedNewton.normalizedJet_zero (P : Polynomial ℝ) (n k : ℕ) (hk : k ≤ n) :
  LeanPaper.NormalizedNewton.normalizedJet P n k 0 = P.coeff k / ↑(n.choose k)
LeanPaper.NormalizedNewton.coefficient_newton {P : Polynomial ℝ} (hP : P.Splits) (k : ℕ) (hk : k + 2 ≤ P.natDegree) :
  P.coeff k / ↑(P.natDegree.choose k) * (P.coeff (k + 2) / ↑(P.natDegree.choose (k + 2))) ≤
    (P.coeff (k + 1) / ↑(P.natDegree.choose (k + 1))) ^ 2
LeanPaper.NormalizedNewton.shifted_coefficient_newton {P : Polynomial ℝ} (hP : P.Splits) (a : ℝ) (k : ℕ)
  (hk : k + 2 ≤ P.natDegree) :
  ((Polynomial.taylor a) P).coeff k / ↑(P.natDegree.choose k) *
      (((Polynomial.taylor a) P).coeff (k + 2) / ↑(P.natDegree.choose (k + 2))) ≤
    (((Polynomial.taylor a) P).coeff (k + 1) / ↑(P.natDegree.choose (k + 1))) ^ 2
LeanPaper.NormalizedNewton.normalized_shifted_newton {P : Polynomial ℝ} (hP : P.Splits) (a : ℝ) (k : ℕ)
  (hk : k + 2 ≤ P.natDegree) :
  ((Polynomial.taylor a) P).coeff k / Polynomial.eval a P / ↑(P.natDegree.choose k) *
      (((Polynomial.taylor a) P).coeff (k + 2) / Polynomial.eval a P / ↑(P.natDegree.choose (k + 2))) ≤
    (((Polynomial.taylor a) P).coeff (k + 1) / Polynomial.eval a P / ↑(P.natDegree.choose (k + 1))) ^ 2
LeanPaper.NormalizedNewton.actual_primitive_laguerre (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters)
  (hp : ManuscriptChain.Real.Admissible p) (x : ℝ) :
  0 ≤ LeanPaper.NormalizedNewton.laguerre p.h (LeanPaper.HigherDerivativeTuran.primitiveRealPolynomial p) x
LeanPaper.NormalizedNewton.actual_primitive_coefficient_newton (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters)
  (hp : ManuscriptChain.Real.Admissible p) (k : ℕ) (hk : k + 2 ≤ p.h) :
  (LeanPaper.HigherDerivativeTuran.primitiveRealPolynomial p).coeff k / ↑(p.h.choose k) *
      ((LeanPaper.HigherDerivativeTuran.primitiveRealPolynomial p).coeff (k + 2) / ↑(p.h.choose (k + 2))) ≤
    ((LeanPaper.HigherDerivativeTuran.primitiveRealPolynomial p).coeff (k + 1) / ↑(p.h.choose (k + 1))) ^ 2
LeanPaper.NormalizedNewton.actual_primitive_shifted_newton (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters)
  (hp : ManuscriptChain.Real.Admissible p) (a : ℝ) (k : ℕ) (hk : k + 2 ≤ p.h) :
  ((Polynomial.taylor a) (LeanPaper.HigherDerivativeTuran.primitiveRealPolynomial p)).coeff k / ↑(p.h.choose k) *
      (((Polynomial.taylor a) (LeanPaper.HigherDerivativeTuran.primitiveRealPolynomial p)).coeff (k + 2) /
        ↑(p.h.choose (k + 2))) ≤
    (((Polynomial.taylor a) (LeanPaper.HigherDerivativeTuran.primitiveRealPolynomial p)).coeff (k + 1) /
        ↑(p.h.choose (k + 1))) ^
      2
LeanPaper.NormalizedNewton.actual_primitive_centered_newton (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters)
  (hp : ManuscriptChain.Real.Admissible p) (k : ℕ) (hk : k + 2 ≤ p.h) :
  ((Polynomial.taylor (ManuscriptChain.Real.zeta p.weight))
                (LeanPaper.HigherDerivativeTuran.primitiveRealPolynomial p)).coeff
            k /
          Polynomial.eval (ManuscriptChain.Real.zeta p.weight)
            (LeanPaper.HigherDerivativeTuran.primitiveRealPolynomial p) /
        ↑(p.h.choose k) *
      (((Polynomial.taylor (ManuscriptChain.Real.zeta p.weight))
                (LeanPaper.HigherDerivativeTuran.primitiveRealPolynomial p)).coeff
            (k + 2) /
          Polynomial.eval (ManuscriptChain.Real.zeta p.weight)
            (LeanPaper.HigherDerivativeTuran.primitiveRealPolynomial p) /
        ↑(p.h.choose (k + 2))) ≤
    (((Polynomial.taylor (ManuscriptChain.Real.zeta p.weight))
                (LeanPaper.HigherDerivativeTuran.primitiveRealPolynomial p)).coeff
            (k + 1) /
          Polynomial.eval (ManuscriptChain.Real.zeta p.weight)
            (LeanPaper.HigherDerivativeTuran.primitiveRealPolynomial p) /
        ↑(p.h.choose (k + 1))) ^
      2
LeanPaper.OperatorNormDisk.whitened {n : ℕ} (p : ManuscriptChain.Spectral.SymmetricAffinePencil n) :
  Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) ℝ
LeanPaper.OperatorNormDisk.hermitian_norm_eq_eigenvalue_norm {n : ℕ} {B : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) ℝ}
  (hB : B.IsHermitian) : ‖B‖ = ‖hB.eigenvalues‖
LeanPaper.OperatorNormDisk.norm_compatible_factorization {n : ℕ} (p : ManuscriptChain.Spectral.SymmetricAffinePencil n)
  (positive : p.base.PosDef) (direction_nonzero : p.direction.det ≠ 0) :
  ∃ c, ‖c.beta‖ = ‖LeanPaper.OperatorNormDisk.whitened p‖
LeanPaper.OperatorNormDisk.complex_root_distance {n : ℕ} (p : ManuscriptChain.Spectral.SymmetricAffinePencil n)
  (positive : p.base.PosDef) (direction_nonzero : p.direction.det ≠ 0) (z : ℂ)
  (hz : Polynomial.eval₂ (algebraMap ℝ ℂ) z p.detPolynomial = 0) :
  1 / ‖LeanPaper.OperatorNormDisk.whitened p‖ ≤ ‖z - ↑p.center‖
LeanPaper.OperatorNormDisk.complex_root_on_boundary {n : ℕ} (p : ManuscriptChain.Spectral.SymmetricAffinePencil n)
  (hn : 0 < n) (positive : p.base.PosDef) (direction_nonzero : p.direction.det ≠ 0) :
  0 < 1 / ‖LeanPaper.OperatorNormDisk.whitened p‖ ∧
    ∃ z,
      Polynomial.eval₂ (algebraMap ℝ ℂ) z p.detPolynomial = 0 ∧
        ‖z - ↑p.center‖ = 1 / ‖LeanPaper.OperatorNormDisk.whitened p‖
LeanPaper.OperatorNormDisk.actual_radius_eq_inverse_norm (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters)
  (hp : ManuscriptChain.Real.Admissible p) (positive : ManuscriptChain.General.GramPositive p) :
  ManuscriptChain.General.zeroFreeRadius p hp positive =
    1 / ‖LeanPaper.OperatorNormDisk.whitened (ManuscriptChain.Real.manuscriptPencil p)‖
LeanPaper.OperatorNormDisk.actual_root_distance (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters)
  (hp : ManuscriptChain.Real.Admissible p) (z : ℂ) (hz : ManuscriptChain.General.ComplexRoot p z) :
  1 / ‖LeanPaper.OperatorNormDisk.whitened (ManuscriptChain.Real.manuscriptPencil p)‖ ≤
    ‖z - ↑(ManuscriptChain.Real.zeta p.weight)‖
LeanPaper.OperatorNormDisk.actual_exact_norm_disk (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters)
  (hp : ManuscriptChain.Real.Admissible p) :
  0 < 1 / ‖LeanPaper.OperatorNormDisk.whitened (ManuscriptChain.Real.manuscriptPencil p)‖ ∧
    (∀ (z : ℂ),
        ‖z - ↑(ManuscriptChain.Real.zeta p.weight)‖ <
            1 / ‖LeanPaper.OperatorNormDisk.whitened (ManuscriptChain.Real.manuscriptPencil p)‖ →
          ¬ManuscriptChain.General.ComplexRoot p z) ∧
      ∃ z,
        ManuscriptChain.General.ComplexRoot p z ∧
          ‖z - ↑(ManuscriptChain.Real.zeta p.weight)‖ =
            1 / ‖LeanPaper.OperatorNormDisk.whitened (ManuscriptChain.Real.manuscriptPencil p)‖
LeanPaper.OperatorNormDisk.original_root_distance (p : Zetahyperbolic.HankelParams) (k : ℕ) (hk : 1 ≤ k) (z : ℂ)
  (hz : Polynomial.eval₂ (algebraMap ℚ ℂ) z (Zetahyperbolic.Delta_poly p k) = 0) :
  1 /
      ‖LeanPaper.OperatorNormDisk.whitened
          (ManuscriptChain.Real.manuscriptPencil (Zetahyperbolic.ManuscriptCompatibility.toParameters p k))‖ ≤
    ‖z - ↑(ManuscriptChain.Real.zeta (2 * k + 1))‖
LeanPaper.PrimitiveNormDisk.normalization_eval_complex {f : Polynomial ℚ}
  (nf : ManuscriptChain.BasisInvariance.PrimitiveNormalization f) (z : ℂ) :
  Polynomial.eval₂ (algebraMap ℤ ℂ) z nf.polynomial = ↑nf.scale * Polynomial.eval₂ (algebraMap ℚ ℂ) z f
LeanPaper.PrimitiveNormDisk.normalization_complex_root_iff {f : Polynomial ℚ}
  (nf : ManuscriptChain.BasisInvariance.PrimitiveNormalization f) (z : ℂ) :
  Polynomial.eval₂ (algebraMap ℤ ℂ) z nf.polynomial = 0 ↔ Polynomial.eval₂ (algebraMap ℚ ℂ) z f = 0
LeanPaper.PrimitiveNormDisk.primitive_complex_root_iff (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters) (z : ℂ) :
  Polynomial.eval₂ (algebraMap ℤ ℂ) z (ManuscriptChain.GeneralIrrationality.primitivePolynomial p) = 0 ↔
    ManuscriptChain.General.ComplexRoot p z
LeanPaper.PrimitiveNormDisk.primitive_root_distance (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters)
  (hp : ManuscriptChain.Real.Admissible p) (z : ℂ)
  (hz : Polynomial.eval₂ (algebraMap ℤ ℂ) z (ManuscriptChain.GeneralIrrationality.primitivePolynomial p) = 0) :
  1 / ‖LeanPaper.OperatorNormDisk.whitened (ManuscriptChain.Real.manuscriptPencil p)‖ ≤
    ‖z - ↑(ManuscriptChain.Real.zeta p.weight)‖
LeanPaper.PrimitiveNormDisk.primitive_exact_norm_disk (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters)
  (hp : ManuscriptChain.Real.Admissible p) :
  0 < 1 / ‖LeanPaper.OperatorNormDisk.whitened (ManuscriptChain.Real.manuscriptPencil p)‖ ∧
    (∀ (z : ℂ),
        ‖z - ↑(ManuscriptChain.Real.zeta p.weight)‖ <
            1 / ‖LeanPaper.OperatorNormDisk.whitened (ManuscriptChain.Real.manuscriptPencil p)‖ →
          Polynomial.eval₂ (algebraMap ℤ ℂ) z (ManuscriptChain.GeneralIrrationality.primitivePolynomial p) ≠ 0) ∧
      ∃ z,
        Polynomial.eval₂ (algebraMap ℤ ℂ) z (ManuscriptChain.GeneralIrrationality.primitivePolynomial p) = 0 ∧
          ‖z - ↑(ManuscriptChain.Real.zeta p.weight)‖ =
            1 / ‖LeanPaper.OperatorNormDisk.whitened (ManuscriptChain.Real.manuscriptPencil p)‖
LeanPaper.PrimitiveNormDisk.primitive_theorem_3_1 (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters)
  (hp : ManuscriptChain.Real.Admissible p) :
  (ManuscriptChain.GeneralIrrationality.primitivePolynomial p).IsPrimitive ∧
    (ManuscriptChain.GeneralIrrationality.primitivePolynomial p).natDegree = p.h ∧
      0 <
          ManuscriptChain.RealTransfer.evalReal (ManuscriptChain.GeneralIrrationality.primitivePolynomial p)
            (ManuscriptChain.Real.zeta p.weight) ∧
        ManuscriptChain.Real.zeta p.weight = (riemannZeta ↑p.weight).re ∧
          0 < 1 / ‖LeanPaper.OperatorNormDisk.whitened (ManuscriptChain.Real.manuscriptPencil p)‖ ∧
            (∀ (z : ℂ),
                Polynomial.eval₂ (algebraMap ℤ ℂ) z (ManuscriptChain.GeneralIrrationality.primitivePolynomial p) = 0 →
                  z.im = 0 ∧
                    z ≠ ↑(ManuscriptChain.Real.zeta p.weight) ∧
                      1 / ‖LeanPaper.OperatorNormDisk.whitened (ManuscriptChain.Real.manuscriptPencil p)‖ ≤
                        ‖z - ↑(ManuscriptChain.Real.zeta p.weight)‖) ∧
              ∃ z,
                Polynomial.eval₂ (algebraMap ℤ ℂ) z (ManuscriptChain.GeneralIrrationality.primitivePolynomial p) = 0 ∧
                  ‖z - ↑(ManuscriptChain.Real.zeta p.weight)‖ =
                    1 / ‖LeanPaper.OperatorNormDisk.whitened (ManuscriptChain.Real.manuscriptPencil p)‖
LeanPaper.PrincipalMinorRank.nonzeroCount {n : ℕ} (beta : Fin n → ℝ) : ℕ
LeanPaper.PrincipalMinorRank.spectralFactor_degree_indicator (center beta : ℝ) :
  (ManuscriptChain.Spectral.spectralFactor center beta).natDegree = if beta ≠ 0 then 1 else 0
LeanPaper.PrincipalMinorRank.degree_eq_nonzeroCount {n : ℕ} {p : ManuscriptChain.Spectral.SymmetricAffinePencil n}
  (w : LeanPaper.SingularSlope.WeakSpectralFactorization p) :

LeanPaper.PrincipalMinorRank.weak_factorization_splits {n : ℕ} {p : ManuscriptChain.Spectral.SymmetricAffinePencil n}

  (w : LeanPaper.SingularSlope.WeakSpectralFactorization p) :
  p.detPolynomial.roots.card = LeanPaper.PrincipalMinorRank.nonzeroCount w.beta

  (p : ManuscriptChain.Spectral.SymmetricAffinePencil n) (positive : p.base.PosDef) :

  LeanPaper.PrincipalMinorRank.nonzeroCount w.beta = p.direction.rank
LeanPaper.PrincipalMinorRank.root_count_le_dimension {n : ℕ} (p : ManuscriptChain.Spectral.SymmetricAffinePencil n)
  (positive : p.base.PosDef) : p.detPolynomial.roots.card ≤ n
LeanPaper.PrincipalMinorRank.zero_direction_has_no_roots {n : ℕ} (p : ManuscriptChain.Spectral.SymmetricAffinePencil n)
  (positive : p.base.PosDef) (hz : p.direction = 0) : p.detPolynomial.natDegree = 0 ∧ p.detPolynomial.roots.card = 0
LeanPaper.PrincipalMinorRank.original_principal_degree_eq_rank (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters)
  (hp : ManuscriptChain.Real.Admissible p) {r : ℕ} (index : Fin r ↪ Fin p.h) :
  (ManuscriptChain.PrincipalMinors.determinant p index).natDegree =
    (LeanPaper.PrincipalMinorRealRoots.originalPencil p index).direction.rank
LeanPaper.PrincipalMinorRank.original_principal_real_roots_card_eq_rank (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters)
  (hp : ManuscriptChain.Real.Admissible p) {r : ℕ} (index : Fin r ↪ Fin p.h) :
  (ManuscriptChain.Real.realPolynomial (ManuscriptChain.PrincipalMinors.determinant p index)).roots.card =
    (LeanPaper.PrincipalMinorRealRoots.originalPencil p index).direction.rank
LeanPaper.PrincipalMinorRank.original_leading_degree_and_root_count (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters)
  (hp : ManuscriptChain.Real.Admissible p) (r : ℕ) (hr : r ≤ p.h) :
  have index := ManuscriptChain.PrincipalMinors.leadingIndex p r hr;
  (ManuscriptChain.PrincipalMinors.determinant p index).natDegree =
      (LeanPaper.PrincipalMinorRealRoots.originalPencil p index).direction.rank ∧
    (ManuscriptChain.Real.realPolynomial (ManuscriptChain.PrincipalMinors.determinant p index)).roots.card =
      (LeanPaper.PrincipalMinorRealRoots.originalPencil p index).direction.rank
LeanPaper.ReciprocalLogDerivative.factorization_logDeriv {n : ℕ} {p : ManuscriptChain.Spectral.SymmetricAffinePencil n}
  (c : ManuscriptChain.Spectral.RootFactorization p) (x : ℝ) (hx : Polynomial.eval x p.detPolynomial ≠ 0) :
  logDeriv (fun t => Polynomial.eval t p.detPolynomial) x = ∑ i, (x - c.root i)⁻¹
LeanPaper.ReciprocalLogDerivative.actual_logDeriv (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters)
  (hp : ManuscriptChain.Real.Admissible p) (x : ℝ)
  (hx : ManuscriptChain.Real.evalQ (ManuscriptChain.Real.delta p) x ≠ 0) :
  logDeriv (ManuscriptChain.Real.evalQ (ManuscriptChain.Real.delta p)) x =
    ∑ i, (x - LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.manuscriptRoots p hp i)⁻¹
LeanPaper.ReciprocalLogDerivative.actual_opposite_gap_sum (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters)
  (hp : ManuscriptChain.Real.Admissible p) (x : ℝ)
  (hx : ManuscriptChain.Real.evalQ (ManuscriptChain.Real.delta p) x ≠ 0) :
  ∑ i, (LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.manuscriptRoots p hp i - x)⁻¹ =
    -logDeriv (ManuscriptChain.Real.evalQ (ManuscriptChain.Real.delta p)) x
LeanPaper.ReciprocalLogDerivative.evalQ_at_rat (P : Polynomial ℚ) (q : ℚ) :
  ManuscriptChain.Real.evalQ P ↑q = ↑(Polynomial.eval q P)
LeanPaper.ReciprocalLogDerivative.rational_logDeriv (P : Polynomial ℚ) (q : ℚ) :
  logDeriv (ManuscriptChain.Real.evalQ P) ↑q = ↑(Polynomial.eval q (Polynomial.derivative P) / Polynomial.eval q P)
LeanPaper.ReciprocalLogDerivative.actual_first_inverse_gap_sum_rational (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters)
  (hp : ManuscriptChain.Real.Admissible p) (q : ℚ)
  (hq : ManuscriptChain.Real.evalQ (ManuscriptChain.Real.delta p) ↑q ≠ 0) :
  ∑ i, (LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.manuscriptRoots p hp i - ↑q)⁻¹ =
    ↑(-Polynomial.eval q (Polynomial.derivative (ManuscriptChain.Real.delta p)) /
        Polynomial.eval q (ManuscriptChain.Real.delta p))
LeanPaper.ReciprocalLogDerivative.actual_log_hasDerivAt (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters)
  (hp : ManuscriptChain.Real.Admissible p) (x : ℝ)
  (hx : ManuscriptChain.Real.evalQ (ManuscriptChain.Real.delta p) x ≠ 0) :
  HasDerivAt (fun t => Real.log (ManuscriptChain.Real.evalQ (ManuscriptChain.Real.delta p) t))
    (∑ i, (x - LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.manuscriptRoots p hp i)⁻¹) x
LeanPaper.ReciprocalNearestRootLimit.finite_power_root_bounds {h : ℕ} (u : Fin h → ℝ) (hu : ∀ (i : Fin h), 0 ≤ u i)
  (j : Fin h) (hj : ∀ (i : Fin h), u i ≤ u j) (n : ℕ) (hn : 0 < n) :
  u j ≤ (∑ i, u i ^ n) ^ (1 / ↑n) ∧ (∑ i, u i ^ n) ^ (1 / ↑n) ≤ ↑h ^ (1 / ↑n) * u j
LeanPaper.ReciprocalNearestRootLimit.finite_power_root_tendsto {h : ℕ} (u : Fin h → ℝ) (hu : ∀ (i : Fin h), 0 ≤ u i)
  (j : Fin h) (hj : ∀ (i : Fin h), u i ≤ u j) :
  Filter.Tendsto (fun m => (∑ i, u i ^ (m + 1)) ^ (1 / ↑(m + 1))) Filter.atTop (nhds (u j))
LeanPaper.ReciprocalNearestRootLimit.reciprocal_atom_negative_half {h : ℕ} (r : Fin h → ℝ) (q : ℝ) (i : Fin h) :
  LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.reciprocalAtom r q i ^ (-1 / 2) = |r i - q|
LeanPaper.ReciprocalNearestRootLimit.pow_negative_root (u : ℝ) (hu : 0 ≤ u) (n : ℕ) (hn : 0 < n) :
  (u ^ n) ^ (-1 / (2 * ↑n)) = u ^ (-1 / 2)
LeanPaper.ReciprocalNearestRootLimit.reciprocal_negative_root_bounds {h : ℕ} (r : Fin h → ℝ) (q : ℝ)
  (hr : ∀ (i : Fin h), r i ≠ q) (j : Fin h)
  (hj :
    ∀ (i : Fin h),
      LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.reciprocalAtom r q i ≤ LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.reciprocalAtom r q j)
  (n : ℕ) (hn : 0 < n) :
  ↑h ^ (-1 / (2 * ↑n)) * |r j - q| ≤ LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.reciprocalEvenMoment r q n ^ (-1 / (2 * ↑n)) ∧
    LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.reciprocalEvenMoment r q n ^ (-1 / (2 * ↑n)) ≤ |r j - q|
LeanPaper.ReciprocalNearestRootLimit.reciprocal_negative_root_tendsto {h : ℕ} (r : Fin h → ℝ) (q : ℝ)
  (hr : ∀ (i : Fin h), r i ≠ q) (j : Fin h)
  (hj :
    ∀ (i : Fin h),
      LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.reciprocalAtom r q i ≤ LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.reciprocalAtom r q j) :
  Filter.Tendsto (fun m => LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.reciprocalEvenMoment r q (m + 1) ^ (-1 / (2 * ↑(m + 1))))
    Filter.atTop (nhds |r j - q|)
LeanPaper.ReciprocalNearestRootLimit.actual_nearest_root_limit (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters)
  (hp : ManuscriptChain.Real.Admissible p) (q : ℚ)
  (hq : ManuscriptChain.Real.evalQ (ManuscriptChain.Real.delta p) ↑q ≠ 0) :
  ∃ j,
    0 < |LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.manuscriptRoots p hp j - ↑q| ∧
      (∀ (i : Fin p.h),
          |LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.manuscriptRoots p hp j - ↑q| ≤
            |LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.manuscriptRoots p hp i - ↑q|) ∧
        (∀ (n : ℕ),
            0 < n →
              ↑p.h ^ (-1 / (2 * ↑n)) * |LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.manuscriptRoots p hp j - ↑q| ≤
                  LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.reciprocalEvenMoment
                      (LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.manuscriptRoots p hp) (↑q) n ^
                    (-1 / (2 * ↑n)) ∧
                LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.reciprocalEvenMoment
                      (LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.manuscriptRoots p hp) (↑q) n ^
                    (-1 / (2 * ↑n)) ≤
                  |LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.manuscriptRoots p hp j - ↑q|) ∧
          Filter.Tendsto
            (fun m =>
              LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.reciprocalEvenMoment
                  (LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.manuscriptRoots p hp) (↑q) (m + 1) ^
                (-1 / (2 * ↑(m + 1))))
            Filter.atTop (nhds |LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.manuscriptRoots p hp j - ↑q|)
LeanPaper.ReciprocalSecondMoment.polynomial_logDeriv_hasDerivAt (P : Polynomial ℚ) (x : ℝ)
  (hx : ManuscriptChain.Real.evalQ P x ≠ 0) :
  HasDerivAt (logDeriv (ManuscriptChain.Real.evalQ P))
    ((ManuscriptChain.Real.evalQ (Polynomial.derivative (Polynomial.derivative P)) x * ManuscriptChain.Real.evalQ P x -
        ManuscriptChain.Real.evalQ (Polynomial.derivative P) x ^ 2) /
      ManuscriptChain.Real.evalQ P x ^ 2)
    x
LeanPaper.ReciprocalSecondMoment.actual_logDeriv_hasDerivAt (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters)
  (hp : ManuscriptChain.Real.Admissible p) (x : ℝ)
  (hx : ManuscriptChain.Real.evalQ (ManuscriptChain.Real.delta p) x ≠ 0) :
  HasDerivAt (logDeriv (ManuscriptChain.Real.evalQ (ManuscriptChain.Real.delta p)))
    (-LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.reciprocalEvenMoment (LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.manuscriptRoots p hp) x 1) x
LeanPaper.ReciprocalSecondMoment.actual_log_second_hasDerivAt (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters)
  (hp : ManuscriptChain.Real.Admissible p) (x : ℝ)
  (hx : ManuscriptChain.Real.evalQ (ManuscriptChain.Real.delta p) x ≠ 0) :
  HasDerivAt (deriv fun y => Real.log (ManuscriptChain.Real.evalQ (ManuscriptChain.Real.delta p) y))
    (-LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.reciprocalEvenMoment (LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.manuscriptRoots p hp) x 1) x
LeanPaper.ReciprocalSecondMoment.actual_second_moment_formula (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters)
  (hp : ManuscriptChain.Real.Admissible p) (x : ℝ)
  (hx : ManuscriptChain.Real.evalQ (ManuscriptChain.Real.delta p) x ≠ 0) :
  LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.reciprocalEvenMoment (LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.manuscriptRoots p hp) x 1 =
    (ManuscriptChain.Real.evalQ (Polynomial.derivative (ManuscriptChain.Real.delta p)) x ^ 2 -
        ManuscriptChain.Real.evalQ (Polynomial.derivative (Polynomial.derivative (ManuscriptChain.Real.delta p))) x *
          ManuscriptChain.Real.evalQ (ManuscriptChain.Real.delta p) x) /
      ManuscriptChain.Real.evalQ (ManuscriptChain.Real.delta p) x ^ 2
LeanPaper.ReciprocalSecondMoment.actual_second_moment_rational (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters)
  (hp : ManuscriptChain.Real.Admissible p) (q : ℚ)
  (hq : ManuscriptChain.Real.evalQ (ManuscriptChain.Real.delta p) ↑q ≠ 0) :
  LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.reciprocalEvenMoment (LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.manuscriptRoots p hp) (↑q) 1 =
    ↑((Polynomial.eval q (Polynomial.derivative (ManuscriptChain.Real.delta p)) ^ 2 -
          Polynomial.eval q (Polynomial.derivative (Polynomial.derivative (ManuscriptChain.Real.delta p))) *
            Polynomial.eval q (ManuscriptChain.Real.delta p)) /
        Polynomial.eval q (ManuscriptChain.Real.delta p) ^ 2)
LeanPaper.Section2RationalDomains.poleProduct.{u_1} {ι : Type u_1} (nodes : ι → ℚ) (s : Finset ι) : Polynomial ℚ
LeanPaper.Section2RationalDomains.poleProduct_ne_zero.{u_1} {ι : Type u_1} [DecidableEq ι] (nodes : ι → ℚ)
  (s : Finset ι) : LeanPaper.Section2RationalDomains.poleProduct nodes s ≠ 0
LeanPaper.Section2RationalDomains.commonNumerator.{u_1} {ι : Type u_1} [DecidableEq ι] (nodes : ι → ℚ)
  (F : LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.SimplePoleForm ι) : Polynomial ℚ
LeanPaper.Section2RationalDomains.realization_eq_fraction.{u_1} {ι : Type u_1} [DecidableEq ι] (nodes : ι → ℚ)
  (F : LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.SimplePoleForm ι) :
  LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.realization nodes F =
    (algebraMap (Polynomial ℚ) (RatFunc ℚ)) (LeanPaper.Section2RationalDomains.commonNumerator nodes F) /
      (algebraMap (Polynomial ℚ) (RatFunc ℚ)) (LeanPaper.Section2RationalDomains.poleProduct nodes F.support)
LeanPaper.Section2RationalDomains.realization_denom_dvd.{u_1} {ι : Type u_1} [DecidableEq ι] (nodes : ι → ℚ)
  (F : LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.SimplePoleForm ι) :
  (LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.realization nodes F).denom ∣
    LeanPaper.Section2RationalDomains.poleProduct nodes F.support
LeanPaper.Section2RationalDomains.fraction_representation_exists.{u_1} {ι : Type u_1} [DecidableEq ι] (nodes : ι → ℚ)
  (hnodes : Function.Injective nodes) (s : Finset ι) (num : Polynomial ℚ) :
  ∃ q c,
    LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.realization nodes { polynomial := q, support := s, residue := c } =
      (algebraMap (Polynomial ℚ) (RatFunc ℚ)) num /
        (algebraMap (Polynomial ℚ) (RatFunc ℚ)) (LeanPaper.Section2RationalDomains.poleProduct nodes s)
LeanPaper.Section2RationalDomains.range_realization_iff_denom_dvd.{u_1} {ι : Type u_1} [DecidableEq ι] (nodes : ι → ℚ)
  (hnodes : Function.Injective nodes) (f : RatFunc ℚ) :
  f ∈ Set.range (LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.realization nodes) ↔
    ∃ s, f.denom ∣ LeanPaper.Section2RationalDomains.poleProduct nodes s
LeanPaper.Section2RationalDomains.muPoleProduct (M : ℕ) : Polynomial ℚ
LeanPaper.Section2RationalDomains.muPoleProduct_eq (M : ℕ) :
  LeanPaper.Section2RationalDomains.muPoleProduct M = Zetahyperbolic.denominatorPolynomial M
LeanPaper.Section2RationalDomains.muDomain_iff_denom_dvd (f : RatFunc ℚ) :
  f ∈ LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.MuDomain ↔
    ∃ M, f.denom ∣ LeanPaper.Section2RationalDomains.muPoleProduct M
LeanPaper.Section2RationalDomains.tauDomain_iff_denom_dvd (f : RatFunc ℚ) :
  f ∈ LeanPaper.Section2TauRepresentation.TauDomain ↔
    ∃ s, f.denom ∣ LeanPaper.Section2RationalDomains.poleProduct (fun r => ↑r) s
LeanPaper.Section2RationalDomains.fraction_mem_muDomain (M : ℕ) (num : Polynomial ℚ) :
  (algebraMap (Polynomial ℚ) (RatFunc ℚ)) num /
      (algebraMap (Polynomial ℚ) (RatFunc ℚ)) (LeanPaper.Section2RationalDomains.muPoleProduct M) ∈
    LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.MuDomain
LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.SimplePoleForm.{u_1} (ι : Type u_1) : Type u_1
LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.effectiveResidue.{u_1} {ι : Type u_1} [DecidableEq ι]
  (F : LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.SimplePoleForm ι) (i : ι) : ℚ
LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.realization.{u_1} {ι : Type u_1} (nodes : ι → ℚ)
  (F : LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.SimplePoleForm ι) : RatFunc ℚ
LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.sum_extend.{u_1, u_2} {ι : Type u_1} [DecidableEq ι] {A : Type u_2}
  [AddCommMonoid A] (F : LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.SimplePoleForm ι) (t : Finset ι)
  (ht : F.support ⊆ t) (f : ι → ℚ → A) (hf : ∀ (i : ι), f i 0 = 0) :
  ∑ i ∈ F.support, f i (F.residue i) = ∑ i ∈ t, f i (LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.effectiveResidue F i)
LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.realization_extend.{u_1} {ι : Type u_1} [DecidableEq ι] (nodes : ι → ℚ)
  (F : LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.SimplePoleForm ι) (t : Finset ι) (ht : F.support ⊆ t) :
  LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.realization nodes F =
    (algebraMap (Polynomial ℚ) (RatFunc ℚ)) F.polynomial +
      ∑ i ∈ t,
        (algebraMap (Polynomial ℚ) (RatFunc ℚ))
            (Polynomial.C (LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.effectiveResidue F i)) /
          (algebraMap (Polynomial ℚ) (RatFunc ℚ)) (Polynomial.X - Polynomial.C (nodes i))
LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.realization_unique.{u_1} {ι : Type u_1} [DecidableEq ι] (nodes : ι → ℚ)
  (hnodes : Function.Injective nodes) (F G : LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.SimplePoleForm ι)
  (h :
    LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.realization nodes F =
      LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.realization nodes G) :
  F.polynomial = G.polynomial ∧
    LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.effectiveResidue F =
      LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.effectiveResidue G
LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.realization_eq_of_data.{u_1} {ι : Type u_1} [DecidableEq ι] (nodes : ι → ℚ)
  (F G : LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.SimplePoleForm ι) (hp : F.polynomial = G.polynomial)
  (hr :
    LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.effectiveResidue F =
      LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.effectiveResidue G) :
  LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.realization nodes F =
    LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.realization nodes G
LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.realization_eq_iff.{u_1} {ι : Type u_1} [DecidableEq ι] (nodes : ι → ℚ)
  (hnodes : Function.Injective nodes) (F G : LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.SimplePoleForm ι) :
  LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.realization nodes F =
      LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.realization nodes G ↔
    F.polynomial = G.polynomial ∧
      LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.effectiveResidue F =
        LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.effectiveResidue G
LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.moment.{u_1} {ι : Type u_1} (polyMoment : Polynomial ℚ → Polynomial ℚ)
  (poleMoment : ι → Polynomial ℚ) (F : LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.SimplePoleForm ι) : Polynomial ℚ
LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.moment_representation_independent.{u_1} {ι : Type u_1} [DecidableEq ι]
  (nodes : ι → ℚ) (hnodes : Function.Injective nodes) (polyMoment : Polynomial ℚ → Polynomial ℚ)
  (poleMoment : ι → Polynomial ℚ) (F G : LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.SimplePoleForm ι)
  (h :
    LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.realization nodes F =
      LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.realization nodes G) :
  LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.moment polyMoment poleMoment F =
    LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.moment polyMoment poleMoment G
LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.muForm (R : Zetahyperbolic.RatMomentElem) :
  LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.SimplePoleForm ℕ
LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.muRatFunc (R : Zetahyperbolic.RatMomentElem) : RatFunc ℚ
LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.mu_representation_unique (R S : Zetahyperbolic.RatMomentElem)
  (h :
    LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.muRatFunc R =
      LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.muRatFunc S) :
  R.polyPart = S.polyPart ∧
    (fun j => if j ∈ Finset.Icc 1 R.maxPole then R.poleRes j else 0) = fun j =>
      if j ∈ Finset.Icc 1 S.maxPole then S.poleRes j else 0
LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.muFunctionalPoly_representation_independent (k : ℕ)
  (R S : Zetahyperbolic.RatMomentElem)
  (h :
    LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.muRatFunc R =
      LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.muRatFunc S) :
  Zetahyperbolic.muFunctionalPoly k R = Zetahyperbolic.muFunctionalPoly k S
LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.MuDomain : Set (RatFunc ℚ)
LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.muOnDomain (k : ℕ)
  (f : ↑LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.MuDomain) : Polynomial ℚ
LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.muOnDomain_eq (k : ℕ) (R : Zetahyperbolic.RatMomentElem) :
  LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.muOnDomain k
      ⟨LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.muRatFunc R, ⋯⟩ =
    Zetahyperbolic.muFunctionalPoly k R
LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.pulledMoment_representation_independent (k : ℕ) (hk : 1 ≤ k)
  (R S : Zetahyperbolic.RatMomentElem)
  (h :
    LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.muRatFunc R =
      LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.muRatFunc S) :
  LeanPaper.PullbackReflection.pulledMoment k R = LeanPaper.PullbackReflection.pulledMoment k S
LeanPaper.Section2TauRepresentation.tauPolynomial (k : ℕ) : Polynomial ℚ →ₗ[ℚ] Polynomial ℚ
LeanPaper.Section2TauRepresentation.tauPolynomial_monomial (k d : ℕ) (c : ℚ) :
  (LeanPaper.Section2TauRepresentation.tauPolynomial k) ((Polynomial.monomial d) c) =
    Polynomial.C (c * Zetahyperbolic.tauMonomial k d)
LeanPaper.Section2TauRepresentation.finiteForm (es : Finset ℕ) (u : ℕ → ℚ) (rs : Finset ℤ) (v : ℤ → ℚ) :
  LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.SimplePoleForm ℤ
LeanPaper.Section2TauRepresentation.finiteSpanRatFunc (es : Finset ℕ) (u : ℕ → ℚ) (rs : Finset ℤ) (v : ℤ → ℚ) :
  RatFunc ℚ
LeanPaper.Section2TauRepresentation.finiteForm_coefficient (es : Finset ℕ) (u : ℕ → ℚ) (rs : Finset ℤ) (v : ℤ → ℚ)
  (d : ℕ) : (LeanPaper.Section2TauRepresentation.finiteForm es u rs v).polynomial.coeff d = if d ∈ es then u d else 0
LeanPaper.Section2TauRepresentation.tauFiniteSpan_eq_moment (k : ℕ) (es : Finset ℕ) (u : ℕ → ℚ) (rs : Finset ℤ)
  (v : ℤ → ℚ) :
  LeanPaper.PullbackReflection.tauFiniteSpan k es u rs v =
    LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.moment (⇑(LeanPaper.Section2TauRepresentation.tauPolynomial k))
      (Zetahyperbolic.tauPolePoly k) (LeanPaper.Section2TauRepresentation.finiteForm es u rs v)
LeanPaper.Section2TauRepresentation.tauFiniteSpan_representation_independent (k : ℕ) (es fs : Finset ℕ) (u w : ℕ → ℚ)
  (rs ts : Finset ℤ) (v z : ℤ → ℚ)
  (h :
    LeanPaper.Section2TauRepresentation.finiteSpanRatFunc es u rs v =
      LeanPaper.Section2TauRepresentation.finiteSpanRatFunc fs w ts z) :
  LeanPaper.PullbackReflection.tauFiniteSpan k es u rs v = LeanPaper.PullbackReflection.tauFiniteSpan k fs w ts z
LeanPaper.Section2TauRepresentation.TauDomain : Set (RatFunc ℚ)
LeanPaper.Section2TauRepresentation.tauOnDomain (k : ℕ) (f : ↑LeanPaper.Section2TauRepresentation.TauDomain) :
  Polynomial ℚ
LeanPaper.Section2TauRepresentation.tauOnDomain_eq (k : ℕ)
  (F : LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.SimplePoleForm ℤ) :
  LeanPaper.Section2TauRepresentation.tauOnDomain k
      ⟨LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.realization (fun r => ↑r) F, ⋯⟩ =
    LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.moment (⇑(LeanPaper.Section2TauRepresentation.tauPolynomial k))
      (Zetahyperbolic.tauPolePoly k) F
LeanPaper.Section2TauRepresentation.tauOnDomain_eq_finiteSpan (k : ℕ) (es : Finset ℕ) (u : ℕ → ℚ) (rs : Finset ℤ)
  (v : ℤ → ℚ) :
  LeanPaper.Section2TauRepresentation.tauOnDomain k
      ⟨LeanPaper.Section2TauRepresentation.finiteSpanRatFunc es u rs v, ⋯⟩ =
    LeanPaper.PullbackReflection.tauFiniteSpan k es u rs v
LeanPaper.Section2TauRepresentation.reflectedMoment_representation_independent (k : ℕ) (hk : 1 ≤ k) (es fs : Finset ℕ)
  (u w : ℕ → ℚ) (rs ts : Finset ℤ) (v z : ℤ → ℚ)
  (h :
    LeanPaper.Section2TauRepresentation.finiteSpanRatFunc es u rs v =
      LeanPaper.Section2TauRepresentation.finiteSpanRatFunc fs w ts z) :
  LeanPaper.PullbackReflection.reflectedMoment k es u rs v = LeanPaper.PullbackReflection.reflectedMoment k fs w ts z
LeanPaper.Section2TauRepresentation.differenceCorrection (k : ℕ) (es : Finset ℕ) (u : ℕ → ℚ) (rs : Finset ℤ)
  (v : ℤ → ℚ) : Polynomial ℚ
LeanPaper.Section2TauRepresentation.differenceCorrection_eq_moment (k : ℕ) (es : Finset ℕ) (u : ℕ → ℚ) (rs : Finset ℤ)
  (v : ℤ → ℚ) :
  LeanPaper.Section2TauRepresentation.differenceCorrection k es u rs v =
    LeanPaper.Section2RepresentationIndependence.moment (fun p => Polynomial.C (p.coeff (2 * k)))
      (fun r => Polynomial.C (-(↑r ^ (2 * k + 1))⁻¹)) (LeanPaper.Section2TauRepresentation.finiteForm es u rs v)
LeanPaper.Section2TauRepresentation.differenceCorrection_representation_independent (k : ℕ) (es fs : Finset ℕ)
  (u w : ℕ → ℚ) (rs ts : Finset ℤ) (v z : ℤ → ℚ)
  (h :
    LeanPaper.Section2TauRepresentation.finiteSpanRatFunc es u rs v =
      LeanPaper.Section2TauRepresentation.finiteSpanRatFunc fs w ts z) :
  LeanPaper.Section2TauRepresentation.differenceCorrection k es u rs v =
    LeanPaper.Section2TauRepresentation.differenceCorrection k fs w ts z
LeanPaper.Section2TauRepresentation.translatedMoment_representation_independent (k : ℕ) (hk : 1 ≤ k) (es fs : Finset ℕ)
  (u w : ℕ → ℚ) (rs ts : Finset ℤ) (v z : ℤ → ℚ) (hr : ∀ r ∈ rs, r ≠ 0 ∧ r ≠ 1) (ht : ∀ r ∈ ts, r ≠ 0 ∧ r ≠ 1)
  (h :
    LeanPaper.Section2TauRepresentation.finiteSpanRatFunc es u rs v =
      LeanPaper.Section2TauRepresentation.finiteSpanRatFunc fs w ts z) :
  LeanPaper.Section2FiniteDifference.translatedMoment k es u rs v =
    LeanPaper.Section2FiniteDifference.translatedMoment k fs w ts z
LeanPaper.ReciprocalAllOrders.actual_inverseGapSum_rational (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters)
  (hp : ManuscriptChain.Real.Admissible p) (n : ℕ) (q : ℚ)
  (hq : ManuscriptChain.Real.evalQ (ManuscriptChain.Real.delta p) ↑q ≠ 0) :
  LeanPaper.ReciprocalAllOrders.inverseGapSum (LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.manuscriptRoots p hp) (n + 1) ↑q =
    ↑(LeanPaper.ReciprocalAllOrders.rationalMoment (ManuscriptChain.Real.delta p) n q)
LeanPaper.ReciprocalAllOrders.actual_iterated_log (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters)
  (hp : ManuscriptChain.Real.Admissible p) (n : ℕ) (x : ℝ)
  (hx : ManuscriptChain.Real.evalQ (ManuscriptChain.Real.delta p) x ≠ 0) :
  iteratedDeriv (n + 1) (fun y => Real.log (ManuscriptChain.Real.evalQ (ManuscriptChain.Real.delta p) y)) x =
    -↑n.factorial *
      LeanPaper.ReciprocalAllOrders.inverseGapSum (LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.manuscriptRoots p hp) (n + 1) x
LeanPaper.ReciprocalAllOrders.primitive_iterated_log (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters)
  (hp : ManuscriptChain.Real.Admissible p) (n : ℕ) (x : ℝ)
  (hx : ManuscriptChain.RealTransfer.evalReal (ManuscriptChain.GeneralIrrationality.primitivePolynomial p) x ≠ 0) :
  iteratedDeriv (n + 1)
      (fun y =>
        Real.log (ManuscriptChain.RealTransfer.evalReal (ManuscriptChain.GeneralIrrationality.primitivePolynomial p) y))
      x =
    -↑n.factorial *
      LeanPaper.ReciprocalAllOrders.inverseGapSum (LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.manuscriptRoots p hp) (n + 1) x
LeanPaper.StrictCenteredLaguerre.dispersion_eq_zero_iff {n : ℕ} (v : Fin n → ℝ) :
  LeanPaper.StrictCenteredLaguerre.dispersion v = 0 ↔ ∀ (i j : Fin n), v i = v j
LeanPaper.StrictCenteredLaguerre.actual_laguerre_dispersion (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters)
  (hp : ManuscriptChain.Real.Admissible p) (x : ℝ)
  (hx : ManuscriptChain.Real.evalQ (ManuscriptChain.Real.delta p) x ≠ 0) :
  LeanPaper.NormalizedNewton.laguerre p.h (ManuscriptChain.Real.realPolynomial (ManuscriptChain.Real.delta p)) x =
    ManuscriptChain.Real.evalQ (ManuscriptChain.Real.delta p) x ^ 2 *
      LeanPaper.StrictCenteredLaguerre.dispersion fun i =>
        (x - LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.manuscriptRoots p hp i)⁻¹
LeanPaper.StrictCenteredLaguerre.actual_laguerre_strict_of_distinct_roots (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters)
  (hp : ManuscriptChain.Real.Admissible p) (x : ℝ)
  (hx : ManuscriptChain.Real.evalQ (ManuscriptChain.Real.delta p) x ≠ 0) (i j : Fin p.h)
  (hij :
    LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.manuscriptRoots p hp i ≠ LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.manuscriptRoots p hp j) :
  0 < LeanPaper.NormalizedNewton.laguerre p.h (ManuscriptChain.Real.realPolynomial (ManuscriptChain.Real.delta p)) x
LeanPaper.ActualStrictCenter.theorem_3_7_strict_center (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters)
  (hp : ManuscriptChain.Real.Admissible p) (hh : 2 ≤ p.h) :
  Polynomial.eval (ManuscriptChain.Real.zeta p.weight)
        (Polynomial.derivative (Polynomial.derivative (LeanPaper.HigherDerivativeTuran.primitiveRealPolynomial p))) /
      Polynomial.eval (ManuscriptChain.Real.zeta p.weight) (LeanPaper.HigherDerivativeTuran.primitiveRealPolynomial p) <
    (1 - 1 / ↑p.h) *
      (Polynomial.eval (ManuscriptChain.Real.zeta p.weight)
            (Polynomial.derivative (LeanPaper.HigherDerivativeTuran.primitiveRealPolynomial p)) /
          Polynomial.eval (ManuscriptChain.Real.zeta p.weight)
            (LeanPaper.HigherDerivativeTuran.primitiveRealPolynomial p)) ^
        2
LeanPaper.Section4ComplexComparison.polynomial_comparison (P : Polynomial ℂ) (w z : ℂ) (δ : ℝ) (hδ : 0 < δ)
  (hPw : Polynomial.eval w P ≠ 0) (hsep : ∀ r ∈ P.roots, δ ≤ ‖w - r‖)
  (hclose : ‖z - w‖ ≤ δ / (8 * (↑P.natDegree + 1))) :
  Polynomial.eval z P ≠ 0 ∧ ‖Polynomial.eval z P‖ ≤ 2 * ‖Polynomial.eval w P‖
LeanPaper.Section4ComplexComparison.integer_comparison (P : Polynomial ℤ) (ξ x ρ : ℝ) (K : ℕ)
  (hvalue : ManuscriptChain.RealTransfer.evalReal P ξ ≠ 0)
  (hsep : ∀ r ∈ (Polynomial.map (algebraMap ℤ ℂ) P).roots, Real.exp (-ρ * ↑K) ≤ ‖↑ξ - r‖)
  (hclose : |x - ξ| ≤ ManuscriptChain.Spectral.comparisonRadius P.natDegree (Real.exp (ρ * ↑K))) :
  ManuscriptChain.RealTransfer.evalReal P x ≠ 0 ∧
    |ManuscriptChain.RealTransfer.evalReal P x| ≤ 2 * |ManuscriptChain.RealTransfer.evalReal P ξ|
LeanPaper.Section4AlgebraicBudget.eventually_conjugate_budget (Q : ℕ → Polynomial ℤ) (d : ℕ) (lam ε L c : ℝ)
  (hlam : 0 ≤ lam) (hL : 0 ≤ L) (hc : 0 < c) (hgap : ↑(d - 1) * L + lam / c < ε)
  (hdegree : ∀ᶠ (K : ℕ) in Filter.atTop, ↑(Q K).natDegree ≤ lam * ↑K)
  (hheight : ∀ᶠ (K : ℕ) in Filter.atTop, LeanPaper.Section4Resultant.naiveHeight (Q K) ≤ Real.exp (L * ↑K ^ 2)) :
  ∀ᶠ (B : ℕ) in Filter.atTop,
    ∀ m ≤ d,
      2 * Real.exp (-ε * ↑(LeanPaper.Section4IndexSelection.gridIndex 1 c B) ^ 2) *
            ((↑(Q (LeanPaper.Section4IndexSelection.gridIndex 1 c B)).natDegree + 1) *
                LeanPaper.Section4Resultant.naiveHeight (Q (LeanPaper.Section4IndexSelection.gridIndex 1 c B))) ^
              (m - 1) *
          (√(↑m + 1) * ↑B) ^ (Q (LeanPaper.Section4IndexSelection.gridIndex 1 c B)).natDegree <
        1
LeanPaper.Section4TransferComplete.spectral_transfer_lattice (Q : ℕ → Polynomial ℤ) (d0 : ℕ) (hd0 : 0 < d0)
  (ξ lam ε ρ μ : ℝ) (hlam : 0 < lam) (hε : 0 < ε) (hρ : 0 < ρ) (hμ : lam * ρ / ε < μ)
  (hdegree : ∀ᶠ (n : ℕ) in Filter.atTop, ↑(Q (d0 * n)).natDegree ≤ lam * ↑(d0 * n))
  (hvalues :
    ∀ᶠ (n : ℕ) in Filter.atTop,
      ManuscriptChain.RealTransfer.evalReal (Q (d0 * n)) ξ ≠ 0 ∧
        |ManuscriptChain.RealTransfer.evalReal (Q (d0 * n)) ξ| ≤ Real.exp (-ε * ↑(d0 * n) ^ 2))
  (hroots :
    ∀ᶠ (n : ℕ) in Filter.atTop,
      ∀ r ∈ (Polynomial.map (algebraMap ℤ ℂ) (Q (d0 * n))).roots, Real.exp (-ρ * ↑(d0 * n)) ≤ ‖↑ξ - r‖) :
  ∃ b₀, ∀ (a : ℤ) (b : ℕ), 0 < b → b₀ ≤ b → ↑b ^ (-μ) < |ξ - ↑a / ↑b|

  (hlam : 0 < lam) (hε : 0 < ε) (hρ : 0 < ρ) (hμ : lam * ρ / ε < μ)
  (hdegree : ∀ᶠ (K : ℕ) in Filter.atTop, ↑(Q K).natDegree ≤ lam * ↑K)

      ManuscriptChain.RealTransfer.evalReal (Q K) ξ ≠ 0 ∧
        |ManuscriptChain.RealTransfer.evalReal (Q K) ξ| ≤ Real.exp (-ε * ↑K ^ 2))
  (hroots :
    ∀ᶠ (K : ℕ) in Filter.atTop, ∀ r ∈ (Polynomial.map (algebraMap ℤ ℂ) (Q K)).roots, Real.exp (-ρ * ↑K) ≤ ‖↑ξ - r‖) :
  ∃ b₀, ∀ (a : ℤ) (b : ℕ), 0 < b → b₀ ≤ b → ↑b ^ (-μ) < |ξ - ↑a / ↑b|

LeanPaper.Section4AlgebraicLimsup.algebraic_transfer_limsup (Q : ℕ → Polynomial ℤ) (d : ℕ) (hd : 1 ≤ d)
  (ξ lam ε ρ Λ μ : ℝ) (hlam : 0 < lam) (hρ : 0 < ρ) (hgap : ↑(d - 1) * Λ < ε)
  (hμ : lam * ρ * ↑d / (ε - ↑(d - 1) * Λ) < μ) (hdegree : ∀ᶠ (K : ℕ) in Filter.atTop, ↑(Q K).natDegree ≤ lam * ↑K)
  (hvalues :
    ∀ᶠ (K : ℕ) in Filter.atTop,
      0 < ‖Polynomial.eval (↑ξ) (LeanPaper.Section4Resultant.complexPolynomial (Q K))‖ ∧
        ‖Polynomial.eval (↑ξ) (LeanPaper.Section4Resultant.complexPolynomial (Q K))‖ ≤ Real.exp (-ε * ↑K ^ 2))
  (hroots :
    ∀ᶠ (K : ℕ) in Filter.atTop,
      ∀ r ∈ (LeanPaper.Section4Resultant.complexPolynomial (Q K)).roots, Real.exp (-ρ * ↑K) ≤ ‖↑ξ - r‖)
  (hheight :
    Filter.limsup (fun K => ↑(Real.log (LeanPaper.Section4Resultant.naiveHeight (Q K)) / ↑K ^ 2)) Filter.atTop ≤ ↑Λ) :
  ∃ B₀,
    ∀ (f : Polynomial ℤ),
      Irreducible (LeanPaper.Section4Resultant.rationalPolynomial f) →
        f.natDegree ≤ d →
          ↑B₀ ≤ LeanPaper.Section4Resultant.naiveHeight f →
            ∀ (α : ℂ),
              Polynomial.eval α (LeanPaper.Section4Resultant.complexPolynomial f) = 0 →
                LeanPaper.Section4Resultant.naiveHeight f ^ (-μ) < ‖↑ξ - α‖
LeanPaper.ActualStrictNewton.actual_coefficient_strict_criterion (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters)
  (hp : ManuscriptChain.Real.Admissible p) (hh : 2 ≤ p.h) (k : ℕ) (hk : k + 2 ≤ p.h) :
  (LeanPaper.HigherDerivativeTuran.primitiveRealPolynomial p).coeff k / ↑(p.h.choose k) *
        ((LeanPaper.HigherDerivativeTuran.primitiveRealPolynomial p).coeff (k + 2) / ↑(p.h.choose (k + 2))) <
      ((LeanPaper.HigherDerivativeTuran.primitiveRealPolynomial p).coeff (k + 1) / ↑(p.h.choose (k + 1))) ^ 2 ↔
    (LeanPaper.HigherDerivativeTuran.primitiveRealPolynomial p).coeff (k + 1) ≠ 0 ∨
      (LeanPaper.HigherDerivativeTuran.primitiveRealPolynomial p).coeff k ≠ 0 ∧
        (LeanPaper.HigherDerivativeTuran.primitiveRealPolynomial p).coeff (k + 2) ≠ 0
LeanPaper.ActualStrictNewton.actual_centered_newton_strict (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters)
  (hp : ManuscriptChain.Real.Admissible p) (hh : 2 ≤ p.h) (k : ℕ) (hk : k + 2 ≤ p.h) :
  ((Polynomial.taylor (ManuscriptChain.Real.zeta p.weight))
                (LeanPaper.HigherDerivativeTuran.primitiveRealPolynomial p)).coeff
            k /
          Polynomial.eval (ManuscriptChain.Real.zeta p.weight)
            (LeanPaper.HigherDerivativeTuran.primitiveRealPolynomial p) /
        ↑(p.h.choose k) *
      (((Polynomial.taylor (ManuscriptChain.Real.zeta p.weight))
                (LeanPaper.HigherDerivativeTuran.primitiveRealPolynomial p)).coeff
            (k + 2) /
          Polynomial.eval (ManuscriptChain.Real.zeta p.weight)
            (LeanPaper.HigherDerivativeTuran.primitiveRealPolynomial p) /
        ↑(p.h.choose (k + 2))) <
    (((Polynomial.taylor (ManuscriptChain.Real.zeta p.weight))
                (LeanPaper.HigherDerivativeTuran.primitiveRealPolynomial p)).coeff
            (k + 1) /
          Polynomial.eval (ManuscriptChain.Real.zeta p.weight)
            (LeanPaper.HigherDerivativeTuran.primitiveRealPolynomial p) /
        ↑(p.h.choose (k + 1))) ^
      2

LeanPaper.Section4Resultant.integer_resultant_product (f Q : Polynomial ℤ) :
  ↑(f.resultant Q f.natDegree Q.natDegree) =
    ↑f.leadingCoeff ^ Q.natDegree *
      (Multiset.map (fun x => Polynomial.eval x (LeanPaper.Section4Resultant.complexPolynomial Q))
          (LeanPaper.Section4Resultant.complexPolynomial f).roots).prod
LeanPaper.Section4Resultant.irreducible_conjugate_product_one_le (f Q : Polynomial ℤ)
  (hf : Irreducible (LeanPaper.Section4Resultant.rationalPolynomial f)) (a : ℂ)
  (hfa : Polynomial.eval a (LeanPaper.Section4Resultant.complexPolynomial f) = 0)
  (hQa : Polynomial.eval a (LeanPaper.Section4Resultant.complexPolynomial Q) ≠ 0) :
  1 ≤
    |↑f.leadingCoeff| ^ Q.natDegree *
      (Multiset.map (fun z => ‖Polynomial.eval z (LeanPaper.Section4Resultant.complexPolynomial Q)‖)
          (LeanPaper.Section4Resultant.complexPolynomial f).roots).prod
LeanPaper.Section4Resultant.integer_mahler_bound (f : Polynomial ℤ) :
  |↑f.leadingCoeff| * (Multiset.map (fun a => max 1 ‖a‖) (LeanPaper.Section4Resultant.complexPolynomial f).roots).prod ≤
    √(↑f.natDegree + 1) * LeanPaper.Section4Resultant.naiveHeight f

LeanPaper.Section4EffectiveBudget.explicit_budgets (n₀ μ : ℕ) (lam ε ρ c σ : ℚ) (hlam : 0 ≤ lam) (hc : 0 < c)
  (hσ : 0 < σ) (hgap : lam / c < ε) (hr : ρ < σ) (hμ : σ * c ≤ ↑μ) (b d : ℕ)
  (hb : LeanPaper.Section4EffectiveBudget.denominatorThreshold n₀ lam ε ρ c σ ≤ b)
  (hd : ↑d ≤ ↑lam * ↑(LeanPaper.Section4IndexSelection.gridIndex 1 (↑c) b)) :
  1 / ↑b ^ μ ≤
      ManuscriptChain.Spectral.comparisonRadius d
        (Real.exp (↑ρ * ↑(LeanPaper.Section4IndexSelection.gridIndex 1 (↑c) b))) ∧
    2 * ↑b ^ d * Real.exp (-↑ε * ↑(LeanPaper.Section4IndexSelection.gridIndex 1 (↑c) b) ^ 2) < 1

  (hlam : 0 ≤ lam) (hc : 0 < c) (hσ : 0 < σ) (hgap : lam / c < ε) (hr : ρ < σ) (hμ : σ * c ≤ ↑μ)

  (hroots : ∀ (K : ℕ), n₀ ≤ K → ∀ r ∈ (Polynomial.map (algebraMap ℤ ℂ) (Q K)).roots, Real.exp (-↑ρ * ↑K) ≤ ‖↑ξ - r‖) :
  ManuscriptChain.RealTransfer.RationalMeasure ξ μ
    (LeanPaper.Section4EffectiveBudget.denominatorThreshold n₀ lam ε ρ c σ)
LeanPaper.Section4AlgebraicTransfer.algebraic_transfer_rates (Q : ℕ → Polynomial ℤ) (d : ℕ) (ξ lam ε ρ L μ : ℝ)
  (hlam : 0 < lam) (hρ : 0 < ρ) (hL : 0 ≤ L) (hgap : ↑(d - 1) * L < ε) (hμ : lam * ρ / (ε - ↑(d - 1) * L) < μ)

          ↑B₀ ≤ LeanPaper.Section4Resultant.naiveHeight f →
            ∀ (α : ℂ),
              Polynomial.eval α (LeanPaper.Section4Resultant.complexPolynomial f) = 0 →
                LeanPaper.Section4Resultant.naiveHeight f ^ (-μ) < ‖↑ξ - α‖

LeanPaper.Section4ConjugateBounds.algebraic_approximation_barrier (f Q : Polynomial ℤ) (a : ℂ)
  (hf : Irreducible (LeanPaper.Section4Resultant.rationalPolynomial f))
  (hfa : Polynomial.eval a (LeanPaper.Section4Resultant.complexPolynomial f) = 0)
  (hQa : Polynomial.eval a (LeanPaper.Section4Resultant.complexPolynomial Q) ≠ 0) :
  1 ≤
    ‖Polynomial.eval a (LeanPaper.Section4Resultant.complexPolynomial Q)‖ *
        ((↑Q.natDegree + 1) * LeanPaper.Section4Resultant.naiveHeight Q) ^ (f.natDegree - 1) *
      (√(↑f.natDegree + 1) * LeanPaper.Section4Resultant.naiveHeight f) ^ Q.natDegree

  ‖Polynomial.eval z P‖ ≤ (↑P.natDegree + 1) * P.supNorm * max 1 ‖z‖ ^ P.natDegree
