  (hfa : a ∈ (LeanPaper.Section4Resultant.complexPolynomial f).roots) :
  |↑(f.resultant Q f.natDegree Q.natDegree)| ≤
    ‖Polynomial.eval a (LeanPaper.Section4Resultant.complexPolynomial Q)‖ *
        ((↑Q.natDegree + 1) * LeanPaper.Section4Resultant.naiveHeight Q) ^ (f.natDegree - 1) *
      (√(↑f.natDegree + 1) * LeanPaper.Section4Resultant.naiveHeight f) ^ Q.natDegree
LeanPaper.CorrectedReciprocalCorollary.corrected_corollary_3_6 (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters)
  (hp : ManuscriptChain.Real.Admissible p) (q : ℚ)
  (hq : ManuscriptChain.RealTransfer.evalReal (ManuscriptChain.GeneralIrrationality.primitivePolynomial p) ↑q ≠ 0) :
  (∀ (m : ℕ),
      ∃ a,
        LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.reciprocalEvenMoment (LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.manuscriptRoots p hp) (↑q)
            m =
          ↑a) ∧
    (∀ (n : ℕ),
        LeanPaper.ReciprocalAllOrders.inverseGapSum (LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.manuscriptRoots p hp) (n + 1) ↑q =
          ↑(LeanPaper.ReciprocalAllOrders.rationalMoment
              (Polynomial.map (Int.castRingHom ℚ) (ManuscriptChain.GeneralIrrationality.primitivePolynomial p)) n q)) ∧
      (∀ (n : ℕ),
          iteratedDeriv (n + 1)
              (fun y =>
                Real.log
                  (ManuscriptChain.RealTransfer.evalReal (ManuscriptChain.GeneralIrrationality.primitivePolynomial p)
                    y))
              ↑q =
            -↑n.factorial *
              LeanPaper.ReciprocalAllOrders.inverseGapSum (LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.manuscriptRoots p hp) (n + 1)
                ↑q) ∧
        (∀ (i : Fin p.h),
            0 <
              LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.reciprocalAtom (LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.manuscriptRoots p hp) (↑q)
                i) ∧
          (∀ (n : ℕ),
              (LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.reciprocalHankel (LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.manuscriptRoots p hp)
                    (↑q) n).PosSemidef ∧
                (Matrix.PosSemidef fun i j =>
                    LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.reciprocalEvenMoment
                      (LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.manuscriptRoots p hp) (↑q) (↑i + ↑j + 1)) ∧
                  (0 <
                        (LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.reciprocalHankel
                            (LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.manuscriptRoots p hp) (↑q) n).det ↔
                      n ≤
                        (LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.atomSupport
                            (LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.reciprocalAtom
                              (LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.manuscriptRoots p hp) ↑q)).card) ∧
                    ((LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.atomSupport
                            (LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.reciprocalAtom
                              (LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.manuscriptRoots p hp) ↑q)).card <
                        n →
                      (LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.reciprocalHankel
                            (LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.manuscriptRoots p hp) (↑q) n).det =
                        0)) ∧
            ∃ j,
              0 < |LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.manuscriptRoots p hp j - ↑q| ∧
                (∀ (i : Fin p.h),
                    |LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.manuscriptRoots p hp j - ↑q| ≤
                      |LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.manuscriptRoots p hp i - ↑q|) ∧
                  (∀ (n : ℕ),
                      0 < n →
                        ↑p.h ^ (-1 / (2 * ↑n)) * |LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.manuscriptRoots p hp j - ↑q| ≤
                            LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.reciprocalEvenMoment
                                (LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.manuscriptRoots p hp) (↑q) n ^
                              (-1 / (2 * ↑n)) ∧
                          LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.reciprocalEvenMoment
                                (LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.manuscriptRoots p hp) (↑q) n ^
                              (-1 / (2 * ↑n)) ≤
                            |LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.manuscriptRoots p hp j - ↑q|) ∧
                    Filter.Tendsto
                      (fun m =>
                        LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.reciprocalEvenMoment
                            (LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.manuscriptRoots p hp) (↑q) (m + 1) ^
                          (-1 / (2 * ↑(m + 1))))
                      Filter.atTop (nhds |LeanPaper.ReciprocalRootMoments.manuscriptRoots p hp j - ↑q|)
LeanPaper.PoleWeightSigns.poleWeight_sign (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters) (hm : 1 ≤ p.m) (a : Fin p.h) :
  SignType.sign (ManuscriptChain.PoleDiagonalization.poleWeight p a) = (-1) ^ (p.N + ↑a)
LeanPaper.Section4IndexSelection.exists_indexBudget_of_rates (Q : ℕ → Polynomial ℤ) (n₀ μ d0 : ℕ) (hd0 : 0 < d0)
  (lam ε ρ c σ : ℝ) (hlam : 0 ≤ lam) (hc : 0 < c) (hσ : 0 < σ) (hdecay : lam / c < ε) (hρσ : ρ < σ) (hμ : σ * c ≤ ↑μ)
  (hdegree : ∀ᶠ (K : ℕ) in Filter.atTop, ↑(Q K).natDegree ≤ lam * ↑K) :
  ∃ b₀,
    ManuscriptChain.RealTransfer.IndexBudget Q
      (fun K => ManuscriptChain.Spectral.comparisonRadius (Q K).natDegree (Real.exp (ρ * ↑K)))
      (fun K => Real.exp (-ε * ↑K ^ 2)) n₀ μ b₀
LeanPaper.Section4IndexSelection.measure_of_rates (Q : ℕ → Polynomial ℤ) (ξ : ℝ) (n₀ μ d0 : ℕ) (hd0 : 0 < d0)
  (lam ε ρ c σ : ℝ) (hlam : 0 ≤ lam) (hc : 0 < c) (hσ : 0 < σ) (hdecay : lam / c < ε) (hρσ : ρ < σ) (hμ : σ * c ≤ ↑μ)
  (hdegree : ∀ᶠ (K : ℕ) in Filter.atTop, ↑(Q K).natDegree ≤ lam * ↑K)
  (hcomparison :
    ManuscriptChain.RealTransfer.LocalComparison Q ξ
      (fun K => ManuscriptChain.Spectral.comparisonRadius (Q K).natDegree (Real.exp (ρ * ↑K))) n₀)
  (hvalues : ManuscriptChain.RealTransfer.Decay Q ξ (fun K => Real.exp (-ε * ↑K ^ 2)) n₀) :
  ∃ b₀, ManuscriptChain.RealTransfer.RationalMeasure ξ μ b₀
LeanPaper.SlopeSignature.actual_normalized_signature (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters)
  (hp : ManuscriptChain.Real.Admissible p) :
  sigPos (LeanPaper.OperatorNormDisk.whitened (ManuscriptChain.Real.manuscriptPencil p)).toQuadraticForm' =
      {a | Even (p.N + ↑a)}.ncard ∧
    sigNeg (LeanPaper.OperatorNormDisk.whitened (ManuscriptChain.Real.manuscriptPencil p)).toQuadraticForm' =
      {a | Odd (p.N + ↑a)}.ncard
LeanPaper.ConsecutiveDerivativeZeros.consecutive_taylor_nonvanishing {P : Polynomial ℝ} (hP : P.Splits) (k : ℕ)
  (hk : k < P.natDegree) (x : ℝ) (hx : Polynomial.eval x P ≠ 0) :
  ((Polynomial.taylor x) P).coeff k ≠ 0 ∨ ((Polynomial.taylor x) P).coeff (k + 1) ≠ 0
LeanPaper.ConsecutiveDerivativeZeros.actual_centered_consecutive_nonvanishing (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters)
  (hp : ManuscriptChain.Real.Admissible p) (k : ℕ) (hk : k < p.h) :
  ((Polynomial.taylor (ManuscriptChain.Real.zeta p.weight))
            (LeanPaper.HigherDerivativeTuran.primitiveRealPolynomial p)).coeff
        k ≠
      0 ∨
    ((Polynomial.taylor (ManuscriptChain.Real.zeta p.weight))
            (LeanPaper.HigherDerivativeTuran.primitiveRealPolynomial p)).coeff
        (k + 1) ≠
      0
LeanPaper.BetaOneMoments.zeroth_moment : ∫ (u : ℝ) in 0..1, 1 / (1 + u ^ 2) = Real.pi / 4
LeanPaper.BetaOneMoments.first_moment : ∫ (u : ℝ) in 0..1, u / (1 + u ^ 2) = Real.log 2 / 2
LeanPaper.BetaOneMoments.eta_at_one_needs_removable_value (zetaAtOne : ℝ) :
  ∫ (u : ℝ) in 0..1, u / (1 + u ^ 2) ≠ (1 - 2 ^ (1 - 1)) * zetaAtOne / 2
LeanPaper.DodgsonCondensation.at_minor_root.{u_1} {n : Type u_1} [Fintype n] [DecidableEq n]
  (A : Matrix n n (Polynomial ℝ)) (U : Matrix n (Fin 2) (Polynomial ℝ)) (D : Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) (Polynomial ℝ))
  (hA : A.IsSymm) (hD : D.IsSymm) (hne : A.det ≠ 0) (x : ℝ)
  (hx : Polynomial.eval x (LeanPaper.DodgsonCondensation.border A U U.transpose D 0 0).det = 0) :
  Polynomial.eval x (Matrix.fromBlocks A U U.transpose D).det * Polynomial.eval x A.det =
      -Polynomial.eval x (LeanPaper.DodgsonCondensation.border A U U.transpose D 0 1).det ^ 2 ∧
    Polynomial.eval x (Matrix.fromBlocks A U U.transpose D).det * Polynomial.eval x A.det ≤ 0
LeanPaper.KreinDimensionObstruction.fixed_K_two_h_two_impossible (q : (Fin 3 → ℝ) → ℝ) :
  ¬∃ S T, Module.finrank ℝ ↥S = 2 ∧ Module.finrank ℝ ↥T = 2 ∧ (∀ v ∈ S, v ≠ 0 → 0 < q v) ∧ ∀ v ∈ T, v ≠ 0 → q v < 0
LeanPaper.StrictNormalizedNewton.coefficient_newton_strict_criterion {P : Polynomial ℝ} (hP : P.Splits)
  (hdist : LeanPaper.DerivativeDistinctRoots.HasDistinctRoots P) (k : ℕ) (hk : k + 2 ≤ P.natDegree) :
  P.coeff k / ↑(P.natDegree.choose k) * (P.coeff (k + 2) / ↑(P.natDegree.choose (k + 2))) <
      (P.coeff (k + 1) / ↑(P.natDegree.choose (k + 1))) ^ 2 ↔
    P.coeff (k + 1) ≠ 0 ∨ P.coeff k ≠ 0 ∧ P.coeff (k + 2) ≠ 0
LeanPaper.StrictNormalizedNewton.normalized_shifted_newton_strict {P : Polynomial ℝ} (hP : P.Splits)
  (hdist : LeanPaper.DerivativeDistinctRoots.HasDistinctRoots P) (x : ℝ) (hx : Polynomial.eval x P ≠ 0) (k : ℕ)
  (hk : k + 2 ≤ P.natDegree) :
  ((Polynomial.taylor x) P).coeff k / Polynomial.eval x P / ↑(P.natDegree.choose k) *
      (((Polynomial.taylor x) P).coeff (k + 2) / Polynomial.eval x P / ↑(P.natDegree.choose (k + 2))) <
    (((Polynomial.taylor x) P).coeff (k + 1) / Polynomial.eval x P / ↑(P.natDegree.choose (k + 1))) ^ 2
LeanPaper.RationalLogJets.iterated_log_rational (P : Polynomial ℚ) (n : ℕ) (q : ℚ)
  (hq : ManuscriptChain.Real.evalQ P ↑q ≠ 0) :
  iteratedDeriv (n + 1) (fun y => Real.log (ManuscriptChain.Real.evalQ P y)) ↑q =
    ↑(LeanPaper.RationalLogJets.rationalJetValue (LeanPaper.RationalLogJets.logJet P n) q)

===== PAPER_PROVEN_CORE.log =====
ManuscriptChain.BasisInvariance.theorem_2_4_canonical_basis_invariance (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters)
  (A B : Matrix (Fin p.h) (Fin p.h) ℚ) (hA : A.det ≠ 0) (hB : B.det ≠ 0)
  (n₀ : ManuscriptChain.BasisInvariance.PrimitiveNormalization (ManuscriptChain.Real.delta p))
  (nAB :
    ManuscriptChain.BasisInvariance.PrimitiveNormalization
      (ManuscriptChain.BasisInvariance.changeBasisPencil (ManuscriptChain.Real.pencil p) A B).det) :
  nAB.polynomial = n₀.polynomial ∨ nAB.polynomial = -n₀.polynomial
ManuscriptChain.PoleDiagonalization.derivativeMatrix_det_ne_zero (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters) :
  (ManuscriptChain.Real.derivativeMatrix p).det ≠ 0
ManuscriptChain.LeadingCoefficientGap.primitivePolynomial_eval_eq (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters) :
  ManuscriptChain.RealTransfer.evalReal (ManuscriptChain.GeneralIrrationality.primitivePolynomial p)
      (ManuscriptChain.Real.zeta p.weight) =
    ManuscriptChain.LeadingCoefficientGap.leadingSize p * ManuscriptChain.LeadingCoefficientGap.determinantRatio p
ManuscriptChain.PoleDiagonalization.delta_leadingCoeff_reweight_add (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters) (k step : ℕ) :
  (ManuscriptChain.Real.delta (ManuscriptChain.PoleDiagonalization.reweight p (k + step))).leadingCoeff =
    (ManuscriptChain.Real.delta (ManuscriptChain.PoleDiagonalization.reweight p k)).leadingCoeff *
      ∏ a, ↑(ManuscriptChain.PoleDiagonalization.poleIndex p a) ^ (2 * step)

===== Paper.Theorem_2_4.log =====
ManuscriptChain.BasisInvariance.theorem_2_4_canonical_basis_invariance (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters)
  (A B : Matrix (Fin p.h) (Fin p.h) ℚ) (hA : A.det ≠ 0) (hB : B.det ≠ 0)
  (n₀ : ManuscriptChain.BasisInvariance.PrimitiveNormalization (ManuscriptChain.Real.delta p))
  (nAB :
    ManuscriptChain.BasisInvariance.PrimitiveNormalization
      (ManuscriptChain.BasisInvariance.changeBasisPencil (ManuscriptChain.Real.pencil p) A B).det) :
  nAB.polynomial = n₀.polynomial ∨ nAB.polynomial = -n₀.polynomial
ManuscriptChain.BasisInvariance.theorem_2_4_constructed_canonical_basis_invariance (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters)
  (A B : Matrix (Fin p.h) (Fin p.h) ℚ) (hA : A.det ≠ 0) (hB : B.det ≠ 0) (hdelta : ManuscriptChain.Real.delta p ≠ 0) :
  have n₀ := ManuscriptChain.BasisInvariance.primitiveNormalization (ManuscriptChain.Real.delta p) hdelta;
  have nAB :=
    ManuscriptChain.BasisInvariance.primitiveNormalization
      (ManuscriptChain.BasisInvariance.changeBasisPencil (ManuscriptChain.Real.pencil p) A B).det ⋯;
  nAB.polynomial = n₀.polynomial ∨ nAB.polynomial = -n₀.polynomial


===== Paper.Theorem_2_5.log =====
ManuscriptChain.PoleDiagonalization.theorem_2_5_lagrange_pole_diagonalization (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters) :
  (∀ (a b : Fin p.h),
      a ≠ b →
        ManuscriptChain.PoleDiagonalization.poleDiagonalPencil p a b =
          C (ManuscriptChain.PoleDiagonalization.poleConstantMatrix p a b)) ∧
    ∀ (a : Fin p.h),
      ManuscriptChain.PoleDiagonalization.poleDiagonalPencil p a a =
          C (ManuscriptChain.PoleDiagonalization.poleWeight p a) *
              (X - C (ManuscriptChain.Real.harmonic p.weight (ManuscriptChain.PoleDiagonalization.poleIndex p a))) +
            C (ManuscriptChain.PoleDiagonalization.poleDiagonalRemainder p a) ∧
        ManuscriptChain.PoleDiagonalization.poleWeight p a ≠ 0


===== Paper.Theorem_3_1.log =====
ZetaHyperbolicPaper.CHAPTER_3_EXACT_DISK (p : ManuscriptChain.Real.Parameters)
  (hp : ManuscriptChain.Real.Admissible p) :
  ∃ radius,
    0 < radius ∧
      (∀ (z : ℂ), ‖z - ↑(ManuscriptChain.Real.zeta p.weight)‖ < radius → ¬ManuscriptChain.General.ComplexRoot p z) ∧
        ∃ z, ManuscriptChain.General.ComplexRoot p z ∧ ‖z - ↑(ManuscriptChain.Real.zeta p.weight)‖ = radius
